## November 22, 2026 — APPROVED JESTER-CHOSEN BINDING TARGETS AND BLIND ACCURACY (PROTOTYPE)

A Jester applying standard Binding **chooses** which arm/leg to bind, or may instead target the **mouth** (silence and prevent actually mouth-dependent actions) or **eyes** (blindness, cannot detect targets by sight). A blinded combatant may attempt an otherwise legal attack if they **correctly guess/deduce the target's tile**, but suffers **−4 Attack accuracy**; this does not restore sight or bypass other attack restrictions. Mouth Binding is not universal anti-magic absent an actual verbal requirement. No extra limbs/parts are implicitly bound by one application; additional multi-part stacking is unresolved. Previously approved arm/leg effects, Pressure penalties to both Reflex/Evasion, advanced Binding and thread counterplay remain. Approved for **HP/d20 replacement prototype**, not yet operative combat. See [Jestercraft](05_Jestercraft.md), [Combat](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md), [Magic](35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md).

---

## November 22, 2026 — APPROVED FREEZE / BINDING STANDARD EFFECTS AND REFLEX PRESSURE (PROTOTYPE)

**Standard Freeze:** halves movement speed and gives **2 Pressure Points**. **Standard Binding:** disables **one limb**, treated as unavailable. An arm cannot use an implement for spellcasting, operate its shield or assist with a two-handed weapon; a bound **leg halves movement and inflicts 1 Pressure Point**. At all tiers, **each effective net PP imposes −2 Evasion AND −2 on Reflex checks**, capped at three PP (−6), when the character becomes Trapped; qualified passive shield blocks continue. Advanced Freeze/Binding still fully incapacitate, subject to their existing removal counters. Affected limb selection, stacked bindings and fractional movement remain open. **Approved HP/d20 replacement-prototype rules**, not yet the operative wound engine. See [unified status reference](35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md), [Jestercraft](05_Jestercraft.md), and [combat](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md).

## November 22, 2026 — APPROVED UNIVERSAL STATUS TEMPLATE AND COUNTERPLAY (HP/D20 PROTOTYPE)

The author approves a **single status schema** for all future conditions and careful conversion of existing ones: attack delivery; damaging/pure buildup; threshold from current ordinary HP (Hero 25%-max floor); standard 1× and advanced 2×; named effects; 3-round refreshed buildup; escalation on new status applications only; immediate downgrade when legally reduced; distinct active-duration clocks; ordinary allied counter default 0.5× standard/0.25× advanced; named full-clear and physical counters; active Shimmer emergency exception; and explicit family-specific exceptions. Incapacitation is advanced-only, Sleep I imposes −2 AC and d20 checks and advanced Sleep lasts one minute; isolated conscious Sleep/Charm have no ordinary self-counter. **One rescuer should delay, several can defeat buildup** as a design balance target, not a guarantee. Older operative exceptions and unresolved named multiplier interactions remain preserved until integrated conversion. [Detailed template and exception table](35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md) · [Combat](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md) · [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).

## November 22, 2026 — LATEST APPROVED D20 / HP-BASED STATUS REDESIGN (REPLACEMENT PROTOTYPE)

Latest author direction: **zero purchased ranks at start**; Attack purchased ranks shared by martial/magical (STR+½DEX martial, INT+½DEX magical), Evasion common; Athletics replaces Movement, Reflex replaces Initiative. Checks `d20+skill >= DC` (5–35), margin ±10 critical, not natural 1/20; attack against `Evasion+10`, AoE Reflex against `spell Attack+10`; attack crits **double HP damage and status buildup**. Pressure gives **−2 Evasion and Reflex per PP**, up to 3; **3 PP automatically Trapped**, while eligible shield blocks remain. Two-action / one-offensive-attack limits continue.

**Replacement status:** resistance threshold uses **current ordinary HP** (Hero floor **25% maximum ordinary HP**); standard threshold **1×**, advanced **2×**. Damaging status spells deal **1× Power damage + 1× buildup**; pure status attacks **3× buildup**, with **×0.5 outgoing Power for advanced-incapacitating spells**. New legal status application refreshes the three-round buildup and checks severity then; falling HP alone does not trigger severity change. Allied counters normally reduce **0.5× effective status HP** for standard and **0.25×** for advanced, with special full clears (e.g., water extinguishes Burning). Sleep I gives **−2 AC and −2 to all d20 checks** without incapacitation; advanced Sleep causes **one minute unconsciousness**, and only an ally can ordinarily wake the sleeping target. Isolated conscious Sleep and Charm targets have no mundane buildup self-cure; Freeze ice and Binding threads can be damaged; living Polymorph's sigil can be healed; psychic effects use Encourage. Existing status weakening/removal downgrades active severity immediately. Shimmer Guard does not routinely block statuses; dormant Hero may break it to reduce defeating status to **1×**. Test targets: **3 successful unprotected damaging hits, 4 advanced-status hits, 5–6 damaging hits with effective MR**, not simulated outcomes. Old 4×/END² rules remain operative until full conversion; Nymbrin/Jester/Encourage special-case migration and resistances still require integration. See [combat prototype](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md), [magical status migration](35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md), [changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).

---
## November 22, 2026 — APPROVED SKILL-ATTRIBUTE/PROFICIENCY ECONOMY AND MOVEMENT (PROTOTYPE ONLY)

**New replacement-prototype author decisions:** baseline **2 SP per Hero level**, plus **2 additional SP per allocated attribute point invested into SKILL**; ordinary SP comes from leveling instead of requiring divine quest rewards. Purchased proficiency ranks have flat prices: **Accuracy 5 SP, Evasion 5 SP, Technique 3 SP, Movement 3 SP, Initiative 3 SP, mundane skills 2 SP**; approved Craft SP ladders, including Jestercraft exceptions, remain unchanged until integrated migration. Purchased general proficiency ranks capped at **10** each; combined permanent purchased+attribute bonus cap **min(20,10+floor(level/10))**, with temporary effects allowed above it. Accuracy derives from full STR+half DEX, Evasion full END+half DEX, Technique **full STR+full DEX**, Movement/Initiative **DEX**. Movement adds **5 ft per +2 effective Movement proficiency**, normal permanent maximum **80 ft**, with approved 30-ft base / 2× Dash / 4× Double Dash and first-tile ZOC retreat exception. Magical Skill checks become eligible design components, with exact contest system and relevant stats still open. **Operative five-stat/wound/SP-quest rules are not yet globally migrated.** See [Combat](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md) and [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).

## November 22, 2026 — HP-PROTOTYPE AUTHOR APPROVAL: +10 ATTRIBUTE ACCURACY/EVASION CAP

The author selects **Option B** for the replacement proficiency-combat system: attribute-derived **Accuracy and Evasion bonuses are each capped at +10**, rather than unlimited. STR contributes to Accuracy, END to Evasion, and DEX contributes half its value to both; tentative `floor((STR+DEX/2)/20)` and `floor((END+DEX/2)/20)` subject to negative-stat/final-rounding integration. Separately the author proposes an overall **permanent skill bonus maximum of `min(20,10+floor(level/10))`**, whose exact interaction with purchased ranks and temporary modifiers requires final clarification. Assistant SP pricing proposals for Accuracy/Evasion and mundane proficiency are **NOT APPROVED**; preserve existing Craft SP ledgers and the operative old combat engine. See [Combat](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md), [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).

## November 22, 2026 — AUTHOR-APPROVED MOVEMENT/INITIATIVE REDESIGN (PROTOTYPE ONLY)

The author approved **separate Movement and Initiative Proficiencies** as part of the developing Skill-based replacement for the SPD attribute. **Base movement 30 ft / 6 tiles; one Dash doubles to 60 ft / 12 tiles; Double Dash doubles again to 120 ft / 24 tiles.** A Double Dash **ignores the first movement tile for hostile zone-of-control purposes to permit retreat**, but not later controlled tiles or independent obstacles. This replaces existing 1.5×/2× Dash only in the proposed replacement system; **operative existing combat remains unchanged** until final system conversion. The author supports a modest free baseline for Weapon Skill/Evasion but has **not approved specific free ranks, level milestones or the 1–20 accuracy equation**. Movement and Initiative proficiency costs/effects and the Skill Point budget remain OPEN. See [Combat](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md) and [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).

## November 22, 2026 — LATEST HP-PROTOTYPE APPROVAL: sleep also RESETS BROKEN Shimmer Guard

In the proposed additive-HP system, **a qualifying sleep ends the 24-hour emergency Shimmer Break lockout early and restores Shimmer HP to full** on waking, with no medical attention required. The 24-hour timeout remains an alternative end to the lockout for a Hero who does not sleep; whether the timeout itself refills Shimmer HP remains open. This explicit latest decision **supersedes earlier same-day HP-prototype statements that sleep cannot end the Break lockout**. The approved **100% ordinary-max-HP reserve**, **51%-of-combined-max-HP emergency trigger**, and separate **Hero ordinary-HP sleep recovery (50% / 100% with medical care)** remain. This is an approved **replacement-prototype decision, not yet operative wound-based canon**. See [Combat](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md) and [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).

## November 22, 2026 — APPROVED HP-PROTOTYPE ARMOR AND RECOVERY (NOT YET OPERATIVE)

**Author-approved design decisions for the planned additive-HP replacement:** Armor subtracts incoming eligible attack damage, possibly to **zero**; armor and weapons compete for shared `Weight capacity=STR+END`; END thus improves HP and available protection, alongside STR's offense/Weight/Technique. Test armor without a separate inherent Speed penalty. Approved **working armor benchmark ladder** by L10/L50/L100 progression reference: Light **10/6, 40/20, 65/35** protection/Weight; Medium **20/12, 75/35, 115/55**; Heavy **35/22, 110/55, 165/85**. These are **test benchmark values, not completed item/material conversions or level gates**. **Sleep restores ordinary folk 10% of maximum ordinary HP, 20% with medical attention; Heroes 50% or 100% with medical attention.** A qualifying sleep fully restores eligible Hero Shimmer HP but **does not bypass the existing 24-hour broken-Shimmer lockout**. Previous approved 100% Shimmer Reserve and 51%-of-combined-max-HP emergency Break remain. Whole-system HP formula and exact rest/rounding/broken-Guard reactivation are still under calibration. **Current wound-based combat remains operative.** See [owning Combat prototype](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md).

---

## November 22, 2026 — AUTHOR-APPROVED SHIMMER GUARD PROTOTYPE (NOT YET OPERATIVE)

For the **proposed additive-HP redesign only**, the author approved a **Shimmer Reserve equal to 100% of maximum ordinary HP** and **emergency Shimmer Break**: while the Guard is intact and dormant, a single attack dealing **at least 51% of maximum COMBINED ordinary+Shimmer HP after armor** instead deals zero HP damage, breaks the Guard, forfeits its unused reserve, and disables Shimmer protection for **24 hours**. Example: 370 ordinary + 370 Guard HP -> break threshold **378** incoming HP damage. The replacement HP formula, armor Weight costs, and final activation/recovery interactions remain provisional. **The existing wound-based rules remain operative.** See [Combat: approved HP-prototype Shimmer Guard](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md).

## November 22, 2026 — LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL: all wound-based combat penalties removed

Wounded, Gravely Wounded, and Hero/Legend Shimmer Guard injury stages impose **no mechanical penalties** to attributes, Combat Speed, attack/Defense Power, hit/evasion, equipment eligibility, or other combat calculations. No percentage, flat, situational, pain-based, or simplified wound penalty replaces the old rules. Pain is narrative only; **Incapacitated** remains the state that ordinarily prevents action. Ordinary three-stage and Hero/Legend five-stage injury progressions remain, with Guard I/II as additional injury capacity rather than reduced-penalty stages. Wounds cannot trigger equipment shortfalls or reduce initiative. See [Combat](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md), [World and Shimmer](01_World_and_Shimmer.md), and [Equipment](09_Equipment_and_Economy.md).

**Combat redesign status:** Attack/Defense Grade conversion and rounded grade-to-injury thresholds remain **provisional test material, not canon**. The current quadratic Power/Defense formulas and impact thresholds remain operative until separately approved. Combat Lab 0.7.0 still implements wound penalties and has no grade-resolution mode, so its full-duel outputs require a separate simulator update before they can test the revised rules.

## November 22, 2026 — LATEST APPROVED: Spell Power governs Thread Grappling initiation AND maintenance

A Jester's Thread Grapple originates at the grappling **Draped tile** (its physical anchor), with **1 MP** base initiation cost; additional legal MP raises **Spell Power**. **Spell Power determines both whether the grapple can be initiated and whether it can be maintained.** This **supersedes** the earlier same-day interpretation requiring a distinct ordinary Technique check for initiation. Ordinary grapple rules apply where compatible and the offensive maneuver limit remains intact; universal Technique is unaffected for other uses. This is distinct from the magical Binding status. Target resistance/opposed Power formula, check timing, range, severing/durability, and multiple grapples remain **OPEN**. Owning [Jestercraft](05_Jestercraft.md#november-22-2026--latest-author-correction-spell-power-initiates-and-maintains-thread-grappling); general [Combat](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md).

## November 22, 2026 — APPROVED: status-inflicting actions are attacks; reusable mana-woven Jestercraft Binding

**Universal combat classification:** Any committed action attempting to **inflict a hostile status effect counts as an ATTACK**, whether or not it deals physical/magical direct damage. It consumes the established maximum of **one offensive attack action per turn**, including failed attempts, and keeps its actual action timing, range, applicable defenses and MP/Channel costs. Ongoing passive effects or actions that remove status are not thereby new independent offensive attacks. See [Combat](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md#november-22-2026--approved-universal-all-active-status-inflicting-actions-are-attacks).

**Jestercraft Binding (LATEST):** Binding is a **nondamaging status ATTACK** with **exactly the same Attack Power as a legal ordinary magical attack made using identical base offensive MP**: `P_Binding(MP)=P_magic_attack(MP)`. Its Draped tile (or Jester with at least one usable Worn Stitch) must lie within **5 feet of the target** and remain legitimately controlled/accessible. **New restraining threads are magically expanded FROM that existing source by expending the attack's mana, rather than cut away from its cloth**: the original Draped tile/rug stays intact and may be used indefinitely for **later separately paid attacks**. No new Worn Stitch is consumed and no free automatic auto-trigger attack occurs just from target entry. **Unlimited distinct captives can be maintained** subject to lawful individual Binding attacks/MP; each captive makes their **own eligible escape**, and success leaves that captive completely free of their restraint while others stay bound. Removing one captive's binding **does not clear the rug tile**. To clear the reusable Draped source, directly remove or destroy the source tile under genuine physical/material rules; what happens to already emitted restraints after destruction of their source remains OPEN. Existing Bound thresholds, technique/cutting counters and status resistances unchanged unless individually revised. Owning [Jestercraft](05_Jestercraft.md#november-22-2026--approved-binding-is-a-mana-powered-status-attack-reusable-draped-sources-and-independent-captives) and [Magical Media](35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md).

## November 22, 2026 — NEW CANON: highest-attribute Technique and one-Stitch Draped sources

**Author-approved universal Technique formula:** `Technique = max(effective STR, effective DEX)`. For all applicable grapple, Feint, zone-of-control, and environmental Unravel contested maneuvers, use the higher effective attribute, not previous **`2×STR+DEX`** (now historical/superseded). The same existing opposed-stat-ratio → Naked-d20 category method applies. High INT alone does not improve Technique; dedicated STR and DEX specialists receive equal direct scaling. Owning [Combat and Shimmer Guard](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md#november-22-2026--latest-approved-universal-technique-formula-highest-str-or-dex).

**Author-approved Jestercraft Draped thresholds:** **One whole Stitch can form FOUR Draped 5-foot environment tiles** through lawful Unravel (one-action handling max `max(1,ceil(full CL/5))` source Stitch-equivalents, source access, physical route, hostile strongest-Technique control contests). **Any Jester wearing at least one usable Worn Regalia Stitch is a Draped source**, permitting binding at ordinary **5-foot melee range** without first creating a separate floor tile. The three-Stitch humanoid torso full defensive covering is a separate requirement and is not needed for mobile Draped Binding. Draped terrain remains in place when it launches restraint fibers, which originate in the environment rather than consuming a fresh Worn Stitch. Whole-Stitch native capacity stays `5+floor(full CL/2)`; **four Draped area tiles do not quadruple the Stitch's defensive Power**. The older proposed `4 Threaded/2 Woven/1 Draped 20-foot tightrope` conversion cannot cap the new explicitly approved **four Draped tiles**; material geometry, damage distribution and per-source sustained binding capacity remain open. Owning [Jestercraft](05_Jestercraft.md#november-22-2026--latest-author-approval-technique-uses-best-strdex-one-stitch-makes-four-draped-tiles-one-worn-stitch-is-draped).

## November 22, 2026 — APPROVED: Unravel throughput, strongest controller, persistent Draped-source Binding

**NEW CANON:** Jestercraft environmental **Unravel and Weave are one-action**, cost **0 additional MP for rearranging already enchanted fabric**, and affect at most `max(1,ceil(full applicable Jestercraft CL/5))` **whole Stitch-equivalents from one eligible accessible source tile** per action; multiple routes can emerge within that capacity. **Moving all Worn Regalia fabric in one action** remains separately exempt from this environmental throughput restriction, but Unraveling it outward is not. Placing invading fabric into real hostile control-zone tiles requires **one opposed Technique check against the SINGLE HIGHEST eligible actually defending controller**, following existing movement reach/geometry; not per defender/tile/rope. A strong warrior can protect genuinely controlled backline positions. Failed contest denies fabric incursion into defended tiles for that action but does not erase separately lawful unopposed deployment. Approved native Stitch stock cap remains `5+floor(full CL/2)`.

**Binding:** Standard Jestercraft Binding is **Short Reach measured from a qualifying Draped source to a target within 5 feet**. Binding uses established environmental fabric and **does not consume/downgrade the Draped/Woven terrain tile or spend a new native Stitch**; environmental silk persists until actually removed/destroyed/redeployed. Ordinary requisite contact, legal control, attack actions, Power/resistance, MP/CL and Bound-status rules still apply. **Conditional mobile source:** if a Jester's Worn Regalia qualifies as Draped it enables melee-range Binding; exact minimum configuration/number of Stitches for Worn Regalia to count Draped is OPEN. This rule is distinct from Nymbrin/Wakecraft environmental-restraint area Magnitude pricing and is not automatic ranged power. The earlier 4 Threaded / 2 Woven / 1 Draped 20-foot tightropes and per-rope Defense are still **pending numeric proposals**, not made final by this approval. Owning [Jestercraft](05_Jestercraft.md#november-22-2026--approved-unravel-throughput-strongest-controller-resistance-and-terrain-origin-binding).

## November 22, 2026 — Latest Unravel action and density direction

**AUTHOR CORRECTION, controlling over earlier one-rope-per-action text:** Unravel may create **multiple distinct tightropes in ONE action**, limited by actual eligible Stitch/fabric stock **within its targeted source tile**, including the Jester's own Regalia. Whole Stitches remain indivisible resource units and the resulting thread networks are physically persistent, not temporary lengthening buffs. **4 Threaded / 2 Woven / 1 Draped 20-foot tightrope(s) from one whole Stitch** is the current **proposed test geometry, NOT YET CANON**. Candidate durability divides the source Stitch's approved **0.5 × defensive spell Power** among those rope paths (Threaded 0.125×, Woven 0.25×, Draped 0.5× eligible spell Power per rope); overall Defense does not multiply through Unravel. Crossing a tile with an elevated rope does not automatically fill it with obstructive Woven/Draped fabric. Exact density geometry, strength, cost and high-area limits remain open. See [Jestercraft](05_Jestercraft.md#november-22-2026--latest-unravel-action-correction-rope-density-comparison-author-direction--numbers-open).

## November 22, 2026 — Latest Unravel material-accounting correction

**AUTHOR DIRECTION:** Unraveling does **not split one Stitch into fractions** in the resource ledger and does **not apply a temporary lengthening buff**. An enchanted whole Stitch may form an enduring physical network spanning multiple Threaded environmental paths until reclaimed, rewoven, damaged or otherwise affected. **One distinct 20-foot tightrope per action** still applies. A single whole Stitch yielding **four separate 20-foot ropes, one per action**, is Quill's **unapproved numerical benchmark**, not yet final fabric capacity or tensile strength. In a 20-foot-square room, four such parallel ropes would span 16 Threaded tile-path segments; the Jester's source ledger would still record one deployed Stitch. The earlier trial values of ½ Stitch per rope, ⅛ Stitch per tile, and temporary ×2 expansion are superseded and **not canon**. Native cap `5+floor(full CL/2)`, 1 MP to enchant one Stitch equivalent, Threadwalk easier Technique category against zones of control, and one-rope-per-action pacing remain intact. See [Jestercraft Unravel correction](05_Jestercraft.md#november-22-2026--unravel-correction-whole-stitches-enduring-environmental-threads-latest-author-direction).

## November 22, 2026 — Jestercraft Threadwalking and Unravel latest author decisions

**APPROVED:** While genuinely moving/jumping using legal Threadwalking or Threadspring pathways, a Jester gets **one easier Naked-d20 difficulty category on Technique checks to pass through hostile zones of control** (not a blanket Technique or attack bonus). **Unravel is limited to one distinct tightrope deployment per action**, so multiple threads in a room take multiple actions. In the author's 20-foot-square-room test, one straight wall-to-wall tightrope crosses four 5-foot tile segments. **Material multiplication is not numerically canonized:** proposed temporary **×2 effective environmental material yield** would let one enchanted source Stitch form two 20-foot ropes over two actions, allowing a CL1 five-Stitch Jester with three torso Stitches to deploy four ropes over four actions from two spare Stitches. Environmental Threaded/Woven/Draped density costs, expansion material budget, physical rope strength and higher-tier manipulation speed remain open. Owner: [Jestercraft](05_Jestercraft.md#november-22-2026--threadwalk-control-zone-advantage-and-one-rope-unravel-rate-author-decisions-expansion-proposal-open); combat cross-reference: [Combat](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md#november-22-2026--threadwalk-easier-technique-checks-through-control-zones-jestercraft-author-approved).

## November 22, 2026 — LATEST APPROVAL: Jester Channel Limit stock; universal Feint

**NEW CANON superseding earlier test-only proposals below:** Regalia native Stitch capacity is **`5 + floor(full applicable Jestercraft Channel Limit / 2)`**. It holds actual native Stitches, not an unlimited external cloth supply and not a bypass of Channel Limit on separately cast magical workings. An innate **one-action** reposition of **already enchanted Worn Regalia fabric** into a **single chosen bodily-location shield** is **not a new mana-costing spell** and is **exempt from the one-action half-CL casting throughput cap**; newly cast magic remains CL/MP bounded. Existing author-approved fabric defensive scale: **0.5× eligible defensive spell Power per Stitch**, 3 Stitches per ordinary humanoid torso, 8 full body. The still-provisional general `CL=floor(INT/10+Mastery)` is not newly ratified merely by selecting the capacity rule. Existing old `20+floor(Mmax/4)` Stitch capacity is superseded for modernization. Detailed [Jestercraft ownership](05_Jestercraft.md#november-22-2026--approved-channel-limit-derived-native-stitches-and-innate-repositioning).

**Universal combat Feint APPROVED:** **1 action**, plausible false attack; **win an opposed Technique check** (CURRENT: `max(effective STR, effective DEX)`) to force an eligible **prepared defensive action** to commit/trigger despite no real strike. A failed check does not waste the defender's preparation. Feint does not deal damage or automatically delete physical shields; a stationary Jester defense stays positioned where baited, with its existing Defense intact. Does not negate Guard by default. Whether the Feint action consumes the turn's one offensive-attack allowance (proposed to remain nonoffensive setup and allow a subsequent one-action Quick Attack) remains **OPEN**; new timing/geometry edge cases still need calibration. [Combat owning rule](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md#november-22-2026--feint-one-action-technique-counter-to-prepared-defense-author-approved) and [Magical Media cross-reference](35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md#november-22-2026--prepared-magical-defenses-can-be-feinted-approved-cross-reference).

## November 22, 2026 — Jestercraft Stitch protection: latest approved direction

**APPROVED:** Every protecting Stitch provides **one-half of applicable defensive spell Power as ablative barrier Defense** (defensive Power formula itself unresolved). Ordinary reference torso covering requires **3 Stitches**; reference whole-body covering **8** (head1, torso3, arms2, legs2). Reinforce protected regions by allocating extra Stitches, within available materials and lawful magic. The author selects **Channel Limit-derived native Stitch capacity** as the preferred progression direction, superseding the proposed independent purchased-upgrade Stitch capacity. **Exact cap formula not approved**; `C=5+floor(CL/2)` is an illustrative test candidate, not canonical progression. A specialized legendary Jester should still manage at least four fully reinforced torso coverings (12 torso Stitches), with full clothing plus that protection consuming 17. ## November 22, 2026 — Jestercraft guarded fabric and enchantment: author directions

**LATEST:** One combat action rearranges Worn Stitches into a **stationary protective barrier for one specified body location**, intercepting all incoming attacks at that location until consumed, not magically following other attack angles. Author favors a stronger **two-action repositioning version**; exact active defense procedure remains open. Layered flexible Regalia is a **finite consumable physical shield**, with partial damage that persists, fully spent layers becoming Frayed, and remaining Attack Power potentially reaching the wearer; **layer × defensive spell Power** is the test starting equation, NOT a finished spell-Power formula. Damaged but not fully Frayed fabric can be retained or expended as Frayed; the author directs that **one spent Stitch repairs all partial damage to any currently Worn damaged fabric** (potential multi-stack repair balance concern).

**Enchanted fabric (Jester-specific):** At **1 MP per Stitch-equivalent**, suitable external fabric may be enchanted without first being worn and is eligible to **physically slither back into the Regalia** to replenish stock; does not increase native cap, create cloth freely, or bypass actual touch/distance/Channel Limit requirements. **Around 200% native Stitch count as the combined enchanted-cloth ceiling, worn plus off-body, remains proposed**, not finalized. Native Stitch capacity must advance sufficiently to permit a specialized level-100 Jester to protect their torso with **four layers**. Full numerical upgrade/cost/density calibration remains open. See [Jestercraft migration and stress tests](05_Jestercraft.md#november-22-2026--curtainwall-prepared-shielding-and-enchanted-fabric-resource) and [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).

## November 22, 2026 — Jestercraft migration decisions (author-approved directions; defensive math pending)

**Status:** The following design directions were explicitly approved by the author in conversation. This is a migration addendum, not a completed rewrite of legacy Jestercraft. Preserve the old ledgers as historical references where their mechanical replacements have not been finalized.

- **Rag Doll tier-0 mobility:** Every Jester gains innate, mana-free Threadwalking: balance and travel along even a single suitably supporting taut thread, including thread-covered walls, ceilings, and suspended routes. From a Threaded or denser launch point, Threadspring permits a leap up to **half normal movement speed in any direction**, including vertically, as part of the applicable movement expenditure; it does not award unlimited free jumps. Advanced Rag Doll/D20 hit avoidance remain to be designed.
- **Environmental fabric:** Threaded → Woven → Draped is the approved escalating fabric-density concept. Another qualifying fabric application upgrades Threaded to Woven and Woven to Draped; **Woven tiles are difficult terrain for non-Jesters**. Draped fabric can serve as a required origin for powerful area binding; exact costs, power, deployment rates and other thresholds remain OPEN. Enemy clothing, furnishings, carpeting, and available fur are potential material sources, **not automatically controlled magical fabric**. Conversion requires legitimate contact/technique; helpless victims may have accessible fabric/fur unraveled. Environmental materials do not automatically increase native Regalia capacity.
- **Material contact:** Deployment does **not** require permanent connection to the Regalia, but Regalia fabric must reach the area by lawful physical means, and **the Regalia must touch fabric to transmit mana for each new magical working**. Ongoing spell contact needs are effect-specific, not presumed perpetual.
- **Stitch stock and capacity:** The intended redesigned Regalia may hold far fewer Stitches than the old ledger. Each Regalia has a finite native Stitch holding capacity; collecting material from the environment is a **cheap but not free replenishment**, not an increase to that capacity. A Regalia's applicable **Channel Limit independently restricts how many Stitches may be allocated into one spell**; conversion between CL and Stitch count is OPEN. Physical reclamation should be cheaper than generating new material magically; exact resource costs remain OPEN. A provisional simplified baseline is **seven Stitches for ordinary full humanoid-body coverage** (head/neck 1, torso 2, both arms/hands 2, both legs/feet 2); still subject to Curtainwall balance, layering, and body size.
- **Bound direction:** A physical accumulative binding condition initially restricts limbs, at **4× applicable resistance** allows escape attempts only, and at **12×** leaves the target helpless and unable to attempt self-escape. Slashing attacks directly reduce buildup at full effective eligible cutting Power; Piercing at half, and bites/claws/unarmed tearing and brute force may be viable depending on method. Escape interactions with anatomy, spell duration, status resistance, cutting material, and assistance remain to be fully reconciled. This replaces neither the global status framework nor the separate environmental restraint rules by implication.
- **Class redesign:** Hemweaving's old bespoke purchase tree and Tailor's Measure are to be retired in the migration. Black Masquerade becomes a core mode, Seamcraft a foundational ability, Curtainwall undergoes a numerical redesign, and Rag Doll retains its identity. The author proposes future Jestercraft–Drider racial linkage, more common Driders and a two-legged/six-armed subordinate spiderfolk subspecies; **these lore proposals are NOT yet canon**, and existing Drider racial lore and artifact-bound Regalia remain in effect until explicitly reconciled.
- **Curtainwall proposal, NOT YET APPROVED:** Explore a physical, expendable, actively prepared fabric shield rather than treating all ordinary flexible Regalia as traditional armor. Layered fabric could provide defensive spell Power per layer, with incoming attack Power consuming barrier capacity, fully depleted layers becoming Frayed, and later hardened Regalia granting conventional armor at possible movement cost. Precise formula, partial-layer damage tracking, bypass/overflow, action timing, armor interaction, baseline layer durability and multi-attack behavior are unresolved and must be tested against established combat rules. No numerical replacement is adopted yet.

## November 21, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: NOBLE-SPONSORED MOSSBACK MEAT DAY

**Latest explicit author direction, approved:** In MOST Thornguard / Canopy noble territories, communal Mossback meat feasts (**working conversational label "Meat Day" until an official name is chosen**) are **culturally significant** public events and **partly financed by the local lord**. The customary noble share is **approximately HALF of the portion/meal price** for eligible participants, with **more generous assistance for families experiencing food insecurity**. This is an established **qualitative institutional norm and approximate 50% sponsorship benchmark**, not a universal statutory program, approved calendar, fixed entitlement for every species/status, compulsory feasting, exact lordly tax rate or financially audited subsidy. Vassal/estate budgets, local coverage and eligibility, feast frequency, reimbursement mechanism and higher need-based rates remain **OPEN for later noble finances work**. Preserve local variation and possible paternalistic and politically instrumental sponsorship consistent with Thornguard Oath of Protection; support does not itself prove that marginalized or enslaved inhabitants receive equivalent access.

**Explicit author aesthetic/economic preference:** Mossback steak should remain an accessible community staple despite giant animals' substantial land, labor, survival, and long grow-out costs. Do NOT simply set production costs implausibly low to achieve the desired retail price. Balance **direct natural grazing**, specialized breeding, valuable milk/fur, feasible slaughter/Recall utilization, and lord-funded consumption subsidy. User observes that a singular unusually ambitious fantasy spectacle is easier to sustain if paired with several mundane economically sound supporting systems. This is design philosophy, not a biological/magical rule.

**New numerical illustrations in the companion economy study are not additional canon rates:** [24 Noble Meat Day Subsidy and Household Purchasing Power DRAFT](https://github.com/MyStalker/mystaria-economy/blob/main/24_Noble_Meat_Day_Subsidy_and_Household_Purchasing_Power_DRAFT.md) assumes tentative pre-subsidy retail **3.20** silver accounting units/kg, meaning illustrative resident share **1.60** and noble contribution **1.60** per kg. For an entirely participating 4,000-person town with 0.4kg/week each, noble yearly outlay is **133,120** units for 83,200kg meat, about 52 age-eight animals at illustrative 1,632kg meat fully assimilated per head; excludes special poverty assistance and administrative/slaughter festival overhead. The hypothetical five-member guard household's net groceries including prior approved 10% contingency drop from **1,261.832** to **1,078.792** units/y under that subsidy, raising 3,000-income leftover savings from **43.168** to **226.208** after other 1,695-unit expenditures. The 3.20 retail price, precise weekly portion, survival/yield, total lord revenues and subsidies beyond 50% remain economic test values only.

## November 21, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: LIVING-HARVEST STARSPAWN MILK AND FUR PERSIST

**Latest explicit author ruling, overriding earlier open question:** **Milk and fur harvested from a Starspawn while the donor is still alive remain as independent, persistent materials even when that Starspawn later dies and undergoes Celestial Recall.** Cheese, whey and other products prepared from this living-harvested milk, and garments/textiles made from living-harvested fur, are likewise not reclaimed solely because the source Starspawn dies. This **does not repeal** the existing ~24-hour postdeath reclamation of unassimilated Starspawn **flesh/carcass**; do not exploit living-harvest rules to relabel dead-animal meat as permanent. Physical harvesting/processing costs, nourishment, spoilage, biological production, status of other bodily products, whether dead-removed pelts differ, and possible fraud remain normal open questions. This approval enables stable dairy inventories and longer cheese aging, and viable clothing made from living-harvested Starspawn fur.

**Social implication / author direction:** Fur coats may have an unsettling symbolism, because a coat's origin could involve fur taken from a sapient animal people or from a living Starspawn. Do not automatically claim the consent, origin, law or abuse of any individual garment. **Wool is a comparatively accessible textile alternative**, with species of source, supply and regional norms still to establish. In canon, Starspawn are persons with memories and fear; leaving their harvested fur persistent does not imply consensual or harmless collection.

**Author's economic priority:** Mossback steak should be an affordable, customary food rather than a rare noble indulgence; explicitly investigate communal/village meat-day distribution and appropriate household meat budgets, rather than insisting on the unapproved historical 0.70 silver accounting unit/kg generic-meat basket quote. No single retail price, dietary ratio, village-wide mandatory custom or slaughter yield is thereby approved.

## November 21, 2026 — AUTHOR DIRECTION: MOSSBACK MILK CREDIT AND CANOPY CHEESE INDUSTRY

The author explicitly requests **including commercializable Mossback surplus milk in meat cost accounting** while **preserving milk needed by offspring and communal nursing**. The author also directs that the Canopy diet should eventually account for dairy using the world's FIVE-COLOR MANAGMIN system and that a **cheese industry** should be developed, with Thornguard cheese-steak demand as a playful cultural possibility, not a settled food price. **Do not invent a fixed milk yield, milk quality/color profile, cheese/curd persistence, husbandry spell or cultural price preference.** Starspawn flesh is reclaimed by Celestial Recall after death; **the effect of the source's later death upon previously separated milk, cheese and whey requires explicit decision before long-aged dairy can be presumed viable**. Milk production uses actual maternal nourishment/water/reserves; its sale may lower net meat-production costs only after offsetting milk production/collection expenses.

## November 21, 2026 — APPROVED MOSSBACK DIRECT GRAZING AS DEFAULT PLAINS ECONOMICS

**Latest explicit author approval:** Mossbacks reared on plains routinely **graze naturally occurring wild Sunrush Grass directly**, rather than having every mouthful planted, mechanically harvested, dried, packed, transported and purchased as commercial fodder. **Managed pasture with suitable supplementary forage** is a valid industry model. **Cut-and-carry cultivated feed** remains important for the protected 0–2-year-old nursery, recovering/Blooming females, illness, shortages and nutritional balancing. This clarifies the already approved ecological relationship in which Sunrush spreads aggressively and Mossback grazing restrains it. Costs for grazing access, water, animal protection, natural pasture restoration, Leshy skill and field herding persist; it is NOT a finding that grass is free or that named supplemental plants universally grow naturally at desired densities. A 100ha commercial Sunrush farm remains an approved working benchmark for the *harvested feed channel*, not all animal consumption. **Author confirms affordable Mossback steak as an explicit economic design goal**, rather than automatically relegating it to luxury status.

**Approved as working scenario, NOT universal fixed fees:** Study [20 Natural Grazing and Mossback Feed Price Reconciliation](https://github.com/MyStalker/mystaria-economy/blob/main/20_Natural_Grazing_and_Mossback_Feed_Price_Reconciliation_DRAFT.md) tests wild Sunrush **8 silver accounting units per dry tonne actually grazed**, optionally compatible supplementary pasture **12 per tonne consumed** versus harvested Sunlace **52.36**, and Stonepod **80.37**. Half of the Sunlace-equivalent supplement grazed gives ration **17.663667 units/tonne**, roughly **42.7%** below completely harvested 30.8243 price, *if the feed is consumed that way*. Actual land rights, crop capacity, feed plant names and balanced managmin ratios remain OPEN. New slaughter-age model will distinguish expensive nursery feed from cheap later plains browsing and account for surviving animal count and carcass yield instead of assuming each age has the same feed bill.

## November 21, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: SPECIALIST TIDEKIN WATER SOURCING AND SUNRUSH LABOR STUDY

**Latest direct author confirmation:** ALL Tidekin can move existing water through their Wakecraft at least at an appropriate individual capability level. In addition, **specialized Tidekin Wakecrafters can extract water from underground sources in industrial quantities and can readily purify seawater**, materially expanding viable agricultural water supply. These are specialist capabilities, NOT automatic industrial-scale abilities of all Tidekin. Respect existing six-discipline Wakecraft access, water-medium, power, range, mana, resource and cooperative casting rules where relevant; no specific rank, per-caster liters/day, power source, salinity efficiency, byproduct disposal, aquifer recharge or infinite freshwater production is approved. Do not overstate "readily purify" as costless or automatically available to a basic caster.

Compare source acquisition, purification, conveyance and field distribution as distinct capacities. Tidekin are enslaved worldwide in the setting, with non-wage provisioning, risk and agency still economically relevant.

## November 21, 2026 — AUTHOR-APPROVED CANOPY SPECIALIST LABOR AND TIDEKIN IRRIGATION BENCHMARK

**New author ruling:** Leshy have inherently greater economic value than most ordinary workers in the Canopy agricultural industry because of their specialized botanical/cultivation capacities. Enslaved Leshy may receive no wages and no ordinary meal rations, but **require light, water, living/appropriate soil or substrate and suitable maintenance**; the lack of a wage or meal bill does NOT render their upkeep, replacement, risk, coercion, security or welfare costs literally zero. Do not treat the older hypothetical 5× *planting/plowing* productivity as blanket 5× total farm yield or workforce replacement. Leshy legal standing in the Canopy remains context-dependent, not a declaration that all Leshy there are enslaved.

**Veterinary scarcity:** Nymbrin are not native to the Canopy Kingdoms. Nymbrin specialists offering living alchemy and magical veterinary treatment are scarce/expensive imported professional services, especially around Mossback breeding and delicate seeds. Their exact fees, population and travel routes are OPEN. Their magic still has established resource/Hex costs.

**Tidekin enslavement and irrigation:** The author's setting direction is that **Tidekin are enslaved worldwide**, with Wakecraft-capable Tidekin more productive at manual irrigation than ordinary workers. A **full Wakecraft irrigation workforce can perform approximately HALF the irrigation work/delivery capacity of a FULL aqueduct** over a comparable operating period. This is a relative **1 full workforce : 0.5 full aqueduct** output benchmark, not a Tidekin-per-aqueduct headcount, water creation spell, guaranteed flow volume, equal operating cost, or new Wakecraft ability. Capture existing distinctions between water sourcing, lifting/direction, distribution, rainfall and seasonal deficits. Exact workforce size, aqueduct reference capacity, individual caster spell ranks/MP, transport and irrigation duty cycles OPEN. Aqueduct maintenance/initial capital, water concessions, and Tidekin workforce acquisition/guarding/living needs each have distinct economic costs. A workforce is not literally free merely because wages are withheld. Broad social status does not erase individual agency, local law or all forms of remuneration.

**New unapproved economic thought experiment:** On 100ha Sunrush land producing the previously proposed 12t usable dry forage/ha/year, output is ~1,200t/year, about 2.645m dry pounds; with speculative 940 managmins/dry lb, ~2.487 billion bioavailable managmins/year. At a illustrative *gross* requirement of 4mm delivered rain-or-irrigation-equivalent water/day, the 100ha unit requires 4m L/day **before subtracting rainfall**. An aqueduct sized to supply the entire illustrative deficit could deliver 4m L/day; one defined 'full' Wakecraft irrigation workforce would be **half of that reference capacity, 2m L/day**, requiring supplementation or more crews to cover that identical deficit. These figures are NOT canon water output or mandatory staffing. Dry Sunrush output is only one part of proposed three-crop feed: at 70% Sunrush of a 37,334t/y provisioned total, full 24-mother-integrated network requires ~26,134t/y Sunrush, or ~2,178ha at 12t/ha, equivalent to ~22 such hypothetical 100ha grass farms. The 37,334t estimate includes prior 20% provision buffer. Actual market costs await labor hours, water source, aqueduct construction amortization, land use, wages, control/security and replacement, and non-wage care bills.

## November 21, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: KHARAK RED AFFINITY, MANAGMIN-ONLY NUTRITION, AND ENERGY UNIT

**Explicit canon correction:** Kharak (Rexfolk) have **RED innate mana affinity**, overriding older notes that labeled their Red affinity unsettled. This does **not** approve new Kharak spell ranks or abilities.

**Foundational nutrition rule:** In Mystaria, conventional **calories, protein, vitamins, and nutritional minerals are NOT separate nutritional resources**: dietary **managmins** replace calories/energy and all vitamin/mineral/protein-style nutritional requirements. Do not create parallel ordinary nutrient balances, protein shortages or mineral-deficiency effects. Water and bulk food/plant matter, cultivation inputs, and other material/environmental needs still physically exist; exact biochemistry, food absorption, non-nutritive material and sunlight-to-managmin conversion need later definition. Earth nutritional measurements may be used solely as *out-of-world analogues for calibrating managmin counts*, not as in-world dietary statistics.

**Approved accounting conversion:** **one managmin supplies energy equivalent to one human-food dietary Calorie (1 kilocalorie or 4.184 kJ)**, NOT one small physics calorie. This equivalence defines a unit of food energy, but not a flat pounds-to-managmins conversion: different foods have different managmin densities. The five colors themselves cover an organism's full nutritional functions. The previously proposed **50% own affinity plus 12.5% each of four others** optimal profile and **Moontouched 20% per color** remain the author's active proposed design for discussion, with broad tolerance of non-ideal diets; do not imply exact deficits/duration penalties are already approved.

**New test ingredient, not yet final species values:** Reference 8.7 MJ of metabolizable energy per kilogram dry Napier/elephant grass (real-world study) maps to ~2,079 managmins/kg of **dry Sunrush** under the above conversion, hence ~943 managmins per dry 1 lb or ~189 per fresh 1 lb at assumed 20% dry matter. Round proposed practical reference to **940 per dry lb and 190 per fresh lb**. Earlier draft Sunrush color composition 5/10/65/12/8 (Red/Blue/Green/White/Black) is STILL PROPOSED; at 940 per dry lb yields ~47/94/611/113/75 total 940 rounded with remainder applied. This is NOT an approved ingredient recipe or an official mass/unit price, and fresh-water fraction remains hypothetical. Source: https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC10682400/ .

**Critical supersession:** Earlier economic drafts using dietary calories/protein/minerals as if they were separate Mystarian bodily resources are obsolete for canon; retain only as historical real-world modeling proxies. The working 75kg dry-feed-equivalent/day for a 10t adult could correspond to ~156,000 ingestible managmins/day if the entire ration had Sunrush's experimental dry density; with a proposed 50% solar contribution, the other half would need to be accounted for by explicit magical/photosynthetic managmin production or another valid pathway. The 50% sunlight contribution was an unvalidated model assumption, not proof that sunlight produces edible/usable managmins. Do not assume that plant matter contains calories in addition to managmins.

## November 21, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: MOSSBACK GROWTH AND WORKING FEED BENCHMARKS

Author explicitly approved the numeric Mossback growth curve developed from elephant reference studies: **~1.5kg seed, ~2kg hatch, 350kg age 1, 900kg age 2, 3,000kg age 5, 4,800kg age 8, 7,000kg age 12, 8,800kg age 16, and 10,000kg at full physical maturity age 20** for a representative well-fed Mossback. The separately established reproductive age ~12 years and one-month seed maturation stand; this is NOT a new universal fixed biological weight for every animal, sex or environment. Maternal post-Bloom loss and six-year restoration follow their own approved cycle.

**Approved as the current economic planning model, still empirically adjustable:** Age 0–5 gross 2% / net 1% bodyweight per day dry-feed-energy-equivalent; age 5–12 gross 1.7% / net 0.85%; age 12+ gross 1.5% / net 0.75%; the net amounts rely on an *illustrative* ~50% sunshine-energy offset. **Do not mistake approval of an accounting benchmark for proof that sunlight supplies actual dietary protein, minerals or half of literal dry-matter intake**; precise photosynthetic physiology and feed nutrients remain OPEN. A working 24-breeding-mother +8-support-adult network, with 30 seeds/Bloom, six years recovery plus illustrative quarter-year Bloom, and 75% captive survival to age 20, models ~115 seeds/year and ~86 eventual age-20 adults/year in mature steady state, not immediate growth. Stage-specific survival 90% at 2 and 82% at 12 is an auxiliary *unapproved* interpolation matching approved 75% at 20; it yields ~1,933 standing growing animals +32 adult herd, ~31,112t annual dry-feed equivalent and ~37,334t including 20% feed provision allowance. This specific operation size and age-stage staffing are **examples**, not species biological laws. The former 6t usable forage/ha model corresponds to ~6,222ha complete integrated 20-year productive land, or ~238ha for a sanctuary selling at two years. Labor, crop quality, sunshine percentage, yields, growth variation and market prices remain OPEN. See [Mossback Growth Study](https://github.com/MyStalker/mystaria-economy/blob/main/13_Mossback_Growth_Curve_and_Lifetime_Feed_DRAFT.md) and [Sunrush Supplement Crop Proposal](https://github.com/MyStalker/mystaria-economy/blob/main/14_Sunrush_Forage_and_Supplement_Crop_Models_DRAFT.md).

## November 21, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: SUNRUSH GRASS; MOSSBACK DEVELOPMENT MISINTERPRETATION CORRECTED

**Correction with highest precedence:** The author's "3×" comparison means that competent captivity yields **three times as many Mossback offspring surviving** compared with wild conditions (**75% versus 25% survival to full physical maturity**), **NOT** threefold faster seed development. Seed development remains approximately **one month** in either setting. Any prior suggestion of about ten days in captivity is **REJECTED/SUPERSEDED**. Full-body maturation ~20 years, reproduction eligibility ~12 years, 30 seeds/Bloom and six-year maternal recovery unchanged.

**Approved natural ecology:** **Sunrush Grass** is a native, aggressively spreading, rhizomatous, sun-loving wild grass of Canopy plains and forest clearings. Without sustained grazing it produces dense stands that suppress smaller flora and can interfere with woodland-edge renewal. Mossbacks are among its important natural controls, cropping/trampling the grass and helping maintain open landscapes. Sunrush is prolific **bulk forage**, not a free complete nutritious ration: animals, particularly juveniles and Blooming/recovering mothers, may require supplemental high-quality feed. Sunrush production remains dependent on ordinary light, water, soil nutrients and usable land. Exact yields, mana traits, chemical composition and growth mechanics are **not** yet canon.

**Approved agricultural industry directions:** Specialized breeding sanctuaries, plains rearing/grow-out operations and forest finishing ranches can coexist; Leshy forage and nursery cultivation, Nymbrin living-alchemy veterinary care, Cogmarshal/Windling handling, protected Bloom meadows, seed nurseries and safe Hex-aware infirmaries are supported. Neither the draft estate's numerical feed/staffing/area assumptions nor illustrative sale ages are automatically canonized by approval of these concepts. Good care and predictable feed can make some Mossbacks more accepting of captivity or eventual slaughter, but individuals vary, death remains horrible for many, and no return to the former ranch is guaranteed.

---

## November 21, 2026 — AUTHOR-APPROVED MOSSBACK SIX-YEAR POST-BLOOM RECOVERY AND STAGGERED SEASONS

**Latest explicit author decision:** Following a Mossback mother's Bloom, she regains **most of her fighting/defensive strength in approximately two years**. At this same **two-year** milestone, her milk production returns to **normal amounts** (ordinary baseline quantities remain unquantified). However, restoring enough fat and other bodily reserves for **another Bloom takes the full six years of recovery**. This is a **six-year post-Bloom recovery** benchmark, not merely a vague interval between births; a new Bloom is not assumed before adequate recovery. This supersedes earlier unspecified multi-year recovery guesses, without changing the approved approximately 30 seeds/Bloom or Bloom's approximately one-season hibernation.

**Seasonal group staggering:** Mossback herds **take turns Blooming in seasonal cohorts**, so only a handful of mothers normally enter Bloom together while others can defend the herd, share milk and care for offspring. It is not established that all females in a herd Bloom in one season or that every calendar season has a cohort. Exact season length, cohort size, synchrony mechanism, and natural breeding schedules remain OPEN.

**Economy implications and explicitly illustrative arithmetic:** With approved 30 seeds/Bloom and 25% wild versus 75% competently captive survival to full size, expected eventual full-grown output per maternal Bloom is 7.5 wild or 22.5 captive. If a female completes a Bloom once every six *post-Bloom recovery* years plus the time spent Blooming, approximate long-run annualized output is slightly less than 7.5/6=1.25 wild or 22.5/6=3.75 captive full-grown animals per female-year, after a 20-year maturation pipeline; these are *upper approximations for a six-year cadence*, not a guaranteed yield, and omit maternal mortality, care costs and immature breeders' fertility. A season-long Bloom in addition to the six recovery years lengthens the true cycle. No canon fixed annual production rate is established.

**Preserve distinctions:** Physical maturity ~20 years; reproductive maturity ~12 years. Milk resumes normal at ~2 years post-Bloom, but this does not indicate full bodily mass/fat restoration or readiness to Bloom. Costs, forage, milk volume, biological age limits, cohort staggering, infant survival timing and ranch economics remain OPEN.

---


## November 21, 2026 — AUTHOR-SELECTED MOSSBACK SURVIVAL BENCHMARKS

**Approved planning baseline:** Of the roughly **30 seeds per Bloom**, an estimated **25% survive to full maturity in the wild**, compared with **75% under competent captivity/rearing**. These are typical comparative ecological expectations, not guaranteed outcomes for an individual brood or a mandatory mechanical dice rule. On a per-Bloom expected-value basis, that is **7.5 fully mature wild survivors** versus **22.5 captive survivors** (fractional numbers denote cohort averages, not individual animals). In a four-mother, 120-seed communal Bloom, corresponding expected eventual full-grown survivors are **30 wild** versus **90 captive**. The 75% figure presumes substantial animal husbandry, communal nursing, Leshy tending, disease/pest control, Nymbrin veterinary interventions and appropriate protection against Hex; it is not survival without associated costs.

Mossbacks reach **reproductive maturity at approximately 12 years**, **full physical maturity around 20 years**. These survival percentages are taken here as survival **from seed to full-size age 20**, for comparison with the existing economic model; survival to reproductive age 12 is **not separately quantified**, nor are age-specific death rates. Relative to the previous unapproved exploratory 10–30% band, the 25% wild and 75% captive baselines take precedence. Bloom frequency, maternal recovery, herd composition, veterinary costs and total saleable yield remain OPEN. Do not treat the captive advantage as a free multiplier on net profitability.

---

## November 21, 2026 — AUTHOR DIRECTION: MOSSBACK JUVENILE MORTALITY, CARE AND MATURATION

**Latest author's selected developmental ages:** Mossbacks reach **pollination/reproductive maturity around age 12 years**, and **full adult physical size around age 20 years**. Those are distinct thresholds: reproductive eligibility at 12 does not mean full body mass, maximum production, or automatic practical breeding success. Maintain the prior approved **about 30 ostrich-egg-sized seeds per Bloom**, multiple communal Blooming mothers, seed milk dependence and shared nursing.

**Ecological direction:** Juvenile/seed survival should be **relatively low**, helping explain large broods, but **NO survival percentage has been approved**. Causes of attrition include unequal access to milk (an overconsuming seed can starve smaller runts), pests and parasites, small predators, disease, **even trace Hex affecting developing seeds**, and a sick/dying Blooming mother potentially losing her entire brood. Specific Hex thresholds, disease outcomes and mortality stages are not quantified; do not treat trace exposure as a universal automatic kill mechanic beyond this species' ecological vulnerability.

**Care and husbandry:** Mossback nurseries have a **high need for Nymbrin healers practiced in living alchemy**, alchemical medical remedies, and **Leshy who cultivate the growing young**. Existing Nymbrin Vital Transmutation permits tissue repair and biological intervention under normal access, Power, material and Hex rules; this direction does not create free or Hexless healing. **NEW PROPOSAL, NOT APPROVED:** separate alchemical treatment/waste from vulnerable seed beds to reduce exposure, subject to how Hex transport is ultimately modeled. **NEW ILLUSTRATIVE ECONOMIC TEST ONLY:** evaluate survival bands of 10–30% through full size, without treating 20% or any other value as canon. Survival to age 12 and age 20 may differ significantly.

**Economics:** A 30-seed cohort's usable output is delayed by up to 20 years for full-size livestock, with earlier potentially fertile 12-year animals. Production planning must include juvenile feeding, nursing competition, disease control, alchemical healing costs/Hex disposal, Leshy support, maternal single-point brood failure, mortality and space. Reproductive interval, maternal regeneration and maturity survival remain OPEN. Do not project a guaranteed adult yield or approved numerical milk price.

---


## November 21, 2026 — MOSSBACK BLOOM AND COMMUNAL NURSERIES (AUTHOR APPROVED)

**Mossback reproductive decisions approved:** Pollinated Mossback mothers ordinarily produce **approximately 30 ostrich-egg-sized seeds per Bloom**. Several mothers, typically **a handful**, enter **the Bloom** together. A mother becomes stationary, hibernation-like and essentially defenseless for **about a season**, losing much of her biomass while milk nourishes her developing seeds; the seeds hatch/develop over **roughly one month**. Milk may be harvested in excess of the offspring's needs, or stolen. Walking sprouts can introduce ordinary feed; other herd females share nursing duties, allowing recovering mothers to rest. Mothers finish the process about **half their original size**, with prior author description indicating roughly **half height and half mass**; subsequent recovery is lengthy, with precise time unresolved.

**Approved communal husbandry directions:** Mossbacks strongly defend Blooming females, including unrelated mothers. Mixed-age herds benefit from protective and supplemental nursing roles. Milk is both a renewable product and a reproductive investment. Different specialists can manage guarded breeding sanctuaries, sunlit plains nurseries and forest finishing ranches. Maternal lost mass is not presumed to provide all offspring growth: sunlight, ordinary feed, water and minerals remain meaningful inputs. Exact spore efficacy, juvenile survival/maturity, recovery interval, reproduction frequency, lactation volumes and numerical outputs remain OPEN. The older elephant single-calf assumption and eight-seed example are **not** the current design. The global hypothesis that Starspawn cannot reproduce in the Celestial Realm remains **separately provisional**, not approved by this Mossback-specific decision. See companion [economy continuity handoff](https://github.com/MyStalker/mystaria-economy/blob/main/12_Economy_Continuity_Handoff_2026-11-21.md).

---

## November 21, 2026 — STARSPAWN CELESTIAL BREEDING AND MOSSBACK POLLINATION HYPOTHESES (PENDING REVIEW)

**New author ecological proposals, not yet finalized mechanical species canon:** Starspawn may **reproduce only in Mystaria, not within the Celestial Realm**, with most returning to Mystaria as adults; this supersedes *as a working hypothesis* the older claim of ongoing Celestial Realm births. Existing immortal Astral Hearts prevent straightforward total extinction, though a local habitat can lose a population, and migrants may return to favored habitats without automatic farm-home respawning. **Mossbacks** may reproduce through **pollination**, yielding **herd-sized litters/sprouts**, rather than elephant-like single calves. Mothers may become mostly immobile, solar-fed milk producers, then emerge about **half their prior height and mass** and need **years** to recover. Sprouts are raised rapidly in **plains fields**, then may be moved or captured into forest ranches near the end of their managed lifespan. Detailed numbers, physical feasibility, and other Mossback traits still under user review. See [World and Shimmer](01_World_and_Shimmer.md#immortality-and-celestial-recall) and [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md). Companion [Economy Continuity Handoff](https://github.com/MyStalker/mystaria-economy/blob/main/12_Economy_Continuity_Handoff_2026-11-21.md) records all approved, provisional and speculative details.

## November 21, 2026 — MOSSBACK NAME APPROVED; SPECIES DESIGN UNDER REVIEW

**Mossback** is the approved name for the proposed giant triceratops-like **green Starspawn livestock species**. **Only the name is newly approved:** its further anatomy, herd behavior, spores, feeding, milk, working role and ranching particulars are pending author review. See [World and Shimmer](01_World_and_Shimmer.md), [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md), and the [experimental estate/ranch docket](https://github.com/MyStalker/mystaria-economy/blob/main/10_Treetop_Estate_and_Starspawn_Ranch_DRAFT.md).

## November 21, 2026 — WORLDWIDE IDIOM APPROVED: “GONE LIKE A 25 HOUR STEAK”

**Approved widespread Mystarian expression:** “Gone like a 25 hour steak,” meaning a thing or opportunity vanished before anyone could enjoy or benefit from it. Alludes to Starspawn flesh disappearing when Celestial Recall normally begins after about **24 hours**; the **25th hour is the joke**, not a revised timing rule. Owner: [Languages and Linguistic Customs](12_Languages_and_Linguistic_Customs.md); see [World and Shimmer](01_World_and_Shimmer.md).

## November 21, 2026 — STARSPAWN FLESH VANISHES ON RECALL; DIGESTED NUTRITION REMAINS (LATEST DECISION)

**APPROVED:** At Celestial Recall, **all unassimilated original Starspawn meat vanishes even if severed, frozen, processed or stored**. The victim's already metabolized/assimilated **nutrition remains**, so previously derived nourishment is not retroactively removed. Standard Recall still normally starts around **24 hours postmortem**; distant settlements therefore commonly transport **living stock**, not long-stored flesh. **Red-managmin carnivorous plants** can metabolize some surplus flesh into lasting new plant material with heavy managmin conversion losses; exact conversion yields/timing OPEN. Meat is an important **dietary and cultural staple**, expected to contribute substantial red managmins that are otherwise difficult to obtain (quantified nutrition OPEN). Economic design target: **~2× as scarce, no more than ~4× as expensive versus a yet-to-be-fixed historical/reference meat baseline**, not an established universal coin price or rigid price control. Remaining ambiguity: what exactly constitutes sufficient digestion/assimilation, price comparator, specific meat supply mixes. See [World and Shimmer](01_World_and_Shimmer.md#immortality-and-celestial-recall) and [Canon Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).

## November 21, 2026 — STARSPAWN RECALL DELAY, RETURN, AND CANOPY RANCHES (LATEST AUTHOR DECISION)

**Ordinary death -> approximately 24 hours before Celestial Recall begins**, superseding prior ordinary immediate-Recall language. Corpse use/processing is time-limited; **~8 hours without refrigeration** is the author's practical meat-spoilage benchmark, not an instantaneous safety threshold. The Astral Heart continues to reclaim remaining original physical matter after Recall begins; whether already butchered/preserved/consumed meat remains is **UNRESOLVED**, so no renewable permanent-meat loophole established. **Return to Mystaria can take about a season for determined Starspawn, a year or centuries for those content in the Celestial Realm.** Return costs substantial fat reserves and some muscle; recovery is necessary. The **Celestial Realm is spatially infinite** and houses most Starspawn; its terrifying predators motivate some to seek Mystaria. Death and especially being eaten remain horrific to aware Starspawn. **Canopy ranches** prize recurring nonfatal ingredient producers but often treat flesh-valued intelligent Starspawn with strict confinement, spartan provisions and neglected personhood; dangerous stock may escape in catastrophic fashion. Hunts to replace lost Starspawn are culturally important. Explicit meat persistence, exceptional obliteration behavior, slaughter consent law, and return locations still OPEN. See [World and Shimmer](01_World_and_Shimmer.md#immortality-and-celestial-recall), [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).

## November 21, 2026 — LIVING-TREE CROP SUBSTRATE; DIRECT IRRIGATION

Leshy-integrated Canopy crops can use the **living host tree as growing medium instead of imported soil for most compatible plants**, but must still receive **direct watering**. Soil remains useful for exceptions. Host-tree capacity, botanical compatibility and irrigation engineering remain OPEN. See [Leshen Foundations](38_Leshen_Leshy_Foundations.md) and [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).

---
## November 21, 2026 — EQUATORIAL CANOPY TREETOP FARMS; LESHY CULTIVATION DIRECTION

Canopy Kingdoms lie on the **equator**, with year-round agricultural potential. Normal farmland is **elevated in the sunlit canopies of giant trees**, with Leshy-grown grains, vegetables and roots such as potatoes intertwined with living branches; soil may be hauled from the dark/dangerous ground below. Leshy agricultural Craft direction: poor-soil remediation and fertilizer substitution, pest/blight control, potent planting/plowing (proposed **~5 conventional workers per Leshy on those tasks**) but a **harvest bottleneck**. A powerful Leshy growing watermelon in desert sand is an illustrative ceiling, **not a defined numeric spell**. Actual yield, load, soil/water/energy costs and Craft mechanics OPEN. See [38 Leshen Foundations](38_Leshen_Leshy_Foundations.md) and [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).

---

## November 21, 2026 — LESHY VINES AND TREE-CANOPY URBAN LIVELIHOOD

Leshy have long body vines granting exceptional treetop mobility and useful strength beyond their ~2.5-foot size. Canopy Kingdoms settlements are integrated into **massive tree canopies**; urban Leshy serve living-tree maintenance, decorative gardens, elevated planting and household botany as well as rural agriculture. No vine reach/Strength/Speed/Craft mechanics approved. See [38 Leshen Foundations](38_Leshen_Leshy_Foundations.md) and [Races](02_Races.md).

## November 21, 2026 — LESHY PHOTOSYNTHETIC INPUTS AND SCARLANDS GROW-LIGHT APPROVED

**Leshen/Leshy need sunlight/light, water, and soil for photosynthetic nourishment**. Scarlands alchemists can create **artificial sunlight in controlled greenhouses**, including subterranean cave gardens useful to sun-averse Vren. This does **not** grant Vren immunity to sunlight or determine whether direct artificial light is hazardous to them; precise requirements, costs, and technical implementation OPEN. See [Leshen Foundations](38_Leshen_Leshy_Foundations.md) and [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).

## November 21, 2026 — LESHEN / LESHY FOUNDATION APPROVED (PLAYABLE MECHANICS OPEN)

**Leshen (Leshy)** are very small (~2.5-foot) sapient plant people nourished by photosynthesis rather than ordinary meals, originating chiefly in the **Canopy Kingdoms**, with a plant-manipulating magical Craft to be designed. They are unusually sensitive to **Haze disruption of their magic**, not automatically killed quickly by Haze. Their cultivation is economically important in the Canopy and for scarce food/rare plants in the Scarlands, where valuable Leshy may be confined in secure or subterranean greenhouses. Cross-border trade and contested Thornguard knightly protection/recovery orders are part of this foundation. Exact legal status, knightly order names, Craft rules, production multipliers, nutrition rules, and player-character attributes remain OPEN; do not present a full playable stat block. See [Leshen Foundations](38_Leshen_Leshy_Foundations.md), [Races](02_Races.md), and [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).

## November 21, 2026 — ENVIRONMENTAL RESTRAINT AREA MAGNITUDE COST APPROVED

**Approved:** Spells binding creatures physically through area terrain manipulation (e.g., Nymbrin re-solidified stone or Wakecraft ice) pay **double normal area Magnitude MP**, preserving **Magnitude 0 single target at 1 MP**. **0/I/II/III/IV/V environmental restraint = 1/4/8/20/60/120 MP**, versus ordinary **1/2/4/10/30/60 MP**. No Power penalty: opposed physical/Technique escape instead of status buildup; actual material, anchor, action, casting requirements and durability govern. Existing Pyromancy natural-area exception unchanged. Detailed opposed-stat mapping, initial defense timing and escape-action particulars remain OPEN. See [Magical Media and Contests](35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md) and [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).

## November 21, 2026 — TRANS MOGRIFIED STATUS AND HELPLESS PUDDLES (LATEST AUTHOR CORRECTION)

**Reshaping III + Vital Transmutation living state shift:** A **pure Transmogrified status attack with 2× ordinary eligible spell Power as buildup**, **not** a 2× MP-cost spell. Earlier living-target surcharge is SUPERSEDED. Do not double this again via general State Mastery state-change Power into 4×. **1× applicable SR -> 1 Pressure Point; 4× -> full liquid/gas, helpless and out of combat** while anchored effect lasts. Other characters can bottle or carry separated portions of a helpless liquid victim. At reformation, eligible pieces have only **six seconds** of attraction based on the original spell's finite Power; separated/contained pieces that do not rejoin remain missing. **Loss of ~5% of a transformed creature's original matter causes a wound on reformation**, without allowing precise anatomical targeting (cannot simply 'remove an arm'). Larger losses can cause further wounds or failed reformation, exact scaling OPEN. See [Wetcraft](20_Nymbrin_Wetcraft_and_Hexen_Canon.md) and [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).

## November 21, 2026 — COMPLETE RESHAPING 0–V; STATE MASTERY REFORMATION (LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL)

**Rank 0–V:** **0 Fundamental Transmutation**, **I Tempering** (anchored Hardening/Softening, proposed ½ Power Defense modifier not finalized), **II Articulation** (sustained caster-controlled motion, not independent Autonomy), **III State Mastery**, **IV Magnify/Minimize**, **V Philosopher**. State-shift reversion: when Rank III anchor/Duration ends, eligible separated parts **attract together with finite force governed by spell Power for up to six seconds**, then remain permanently in whatever physical condition results. Burned or chemically transformed portions are not reassembled; minor missing pieces leave gaps; major portions missing can wound or prevent complete reformation. Same physics apply to living targets. Drinking stone converted to water triggers **opposed drinker's END against spell Power; failure Stunned one round** as stone exits. Advanced creature state shifting requires Vital Transmutation and **2× normal spell-equivalent MP**: **Transmogrified 1× SR -> one Pressure Point; 4× SR -> immediately out of combat** while the effect lasts, without bypassing buildup. State-change **×2 eligible Power does not automatically double subsequent reversion projectile damage**. Exact build-up coefficient, doubled MP allocation, impact/wound calculations remain open. See [Wetcraft](20_Nymbrin_Wetcraft_and_Hexen_Canon.md) and [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).

---

## November 21, 2026 — RESHAPING III–V CLARIFICATIONS (LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL)

**III State Mastery:** its ×2 Power applies **only to the state-changing spell effect**, not automatic ×2 to resulting attacks, flooding, or incidental physical harm. **IV Magnify/Minimize:** every 4× size step changes **volume AND mass by a factor of four** (not each linear dimension); further fourfold steps require ×4 the prior step's Power; all changes anchored/temporary. **V Philosopher:** temporary anchored conversion to a sufficiently similar material **usually preserves volume, while mass adjusts to the new material/density**. This rank expressly allows changing mass and chemical identity, rather than conserving original constituents, without permitting unrelated source materials or unanchored permanent conversion. See [Wetcraft](20_Nymbrin_Wetcraft_and_Hexen_Canon.md) and [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md). Reshaping I/II and detailed edge cases remain open.

---

## November 21, 2026 — RESHAPING RANKS III–V SELECTED (LATEST AUTHOR DIRECTION)

**III State Mastery:** eligible liquid/gas shaping and anchored temporary state changes, **×2 effective Power for changing physical state**; reverting on anchor/Duration loss. Living-target change needs separate **Vital Transmutation**, applying **Transmogrified** buildup: **1× SR -> one Pressure Point; 4× SR -> out of combat while the spell lasts**. **IV Magnify/Minimize:** anchored temporary changes to target size/volume/mass, baseline **4× smaller** with each further fourfold step requiring **4× Power**; living target requires Vital Transmutation; reversion can inflict ordinary base spell Power as collision damage, greater situational multipliers only under DM adjudication. **V Philosopher:** anchored temporary conversion to a reasonably similar eligible material type (milk->wine, iron->adamantite permitted; motor oil->wine, trufula->adamantite disallowed); with Vital Transmutation can temporarily polymorph creatures into different creatures. These are author-selected replacements of previous III–V proposals. **I/II remain to finalize;** precise power/status/size geometry, mass and equivalent-exchange reconciliation, material resemblance, delivery, reversion and creature statistics remain OPEN. See [Nymbrin Wetcraft](20_Nymbrin_Wetcraft_and_Hexen_Canon.md) and [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).

## November 21, 2026 — RESHAPING USES ORDINARY SPELL ATTACK POWER (LATEST AUTHOR CORRECTION)

**Approved clarification:** Meaningful Nymbrin Reshaping uses the standard offensive base MP-to-Power curve and **20 MP base-Power limit**, with separate lawful Magnitude/Duration costs, Channel Limit, real material and generated Hex. Rank 0 nonliving Mold may legally form stone spikes for ordinary attacks or hands for eligible grapple attempts, subject to real attack/grapple action and resistance rules; these do not grant free hits, a bonus attack or sustained animation. No independent Reshaping-specific Power-price ladder. Later Reshaping I–V technique examples are **UNAPPROVED** until author selection. See [Wetcraft](20_Nymbrin_Wetcraft_and_Hexen_Canon.md) and [Changelog](10_Canon_Changelog.md).
