## Hearthiron Medium Furnace Burst confirmed — November 9, 2026 (LATEST)

**APPROVED:** Medium and Heavy Hearthiron emit **equal-power Quality-based thermal AoE Furnace Bursts**; Medium reservoir **1 charge**, Heavy **2 charges**. Medium still provides shared warmth; Heavy has more fuel and high-output contact heat. Light specializes in cold-weather survival. Earlier Quill proposal of 3m radius, draft weapon factor, exact Quality Power ladder, and 70%-of-generalist-wound recharge criterion remain **UNAPPROVED PLACEHOLDERS**. Heat-fuel/recharge philosophy and x3 Fire resistance preserved; no blanket ice vulnerability. See Equipment, Smithcraft, Combat and Changelog.



## November 9, 2026 — Hearthiron Medium Furnace Burst shares Heavy damage with smaller reservoir (LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL)

**APPROVED CLASS IDENTITY AND CHARGE COUNTS:** **Medium Hearthiron armor HAS Furnace Burst**, an actual offensive area thermal discharge **of the SAME base Power/damage as the Heavy Hearthiron Furnace Burst at the SAME workmanship Quality**. The two are distinguished by stored uses: **Medium holds ONE Furnace Burst charge**, **Heavy holds TWO Furnace Burst charges** between full replenishments. Medium retains its approved ability to regulate shared radiant warmth, while Heavy retains more stored fuel and strong furnace/contact-heat potential. Medium is not reduced to a glorified lantern; do NOT assign it a weaker burst multiplier solely for being Medium. Light retains its personal survival/thermal comfort identity, with no Furnace Burst established for Light. The author's expectation is that Medium armor commonly combines features associated with Light and Heavy. In this case the difference is endurance/capacity rather than single-burst damage.

**ALREADY APPROVED RECHARGE PHILOSOPHY:** More intense external fire, suitable pyromancy, and sufficiently strong enemy attacks may recharge Hearthiron in battle; ordinary campfire heat needs an unspecified longer time. Thermal exhaustion turns off charged offensive functionality but does not lower base AR or x3 fire resistance. Thermal discharge endangers exposed bystanders and environment. A Furnace Burst expends one stored charge; it is not an extra free attack on top of ordinary action economy and is not a wielder-STR/DEX/INT-scaled weapon attack. Attacks are Quality-oriented rather than attacker-stat-oriented as the author has directed.

**STILL ONLY QUILL'S UNAPPROVED PLACEHOLDER NUMERIC TESTS:** Earlier suggestions to use **3m AoE radius**, **one attack action**, **medium weapon/longsword-equivalent magnitude** with hypothetical **F1.10** and reference Quality I–V attributes **25/35/50/65/80** (producing draft Power **756/1482/3025/5112/7744**), and rapid recharge at approximately **70% of a corresponding reference-generalist's wounding thermal threshold**, are BALANCING PROPOSALS, not a finalized elemental-damage formula or canon numerics. The author has explicitly endorsed **equal Medium/Heavy damage and ONE/TWO charges**, not yet ratified the preceding entire table, radius or recharge percentage. Preserve earlier design goals for AoE thermal damage comparable with a Medium weapon and scaling from equipment Quality rather than attacker's stats. **Elemental Magnitude resolution remains unfinished**; do not incorrectly apply the physical sword Power equation automatically to fire damage, or treat STR/DEX or attack frequency as its scaling mechanism.
## Hearthiron thermal exhaustion replaces ice vulnerability — November 8, 2026 (LATEST)

**AUTHOR APPROVED:** Heat is a finite resource mainly spent on active furnace/radiant effects; dangerous heat discharge can harm allies and environment. Powerful fire, high-intensity pyromancy, or strong enemy fire can refill the reservoir rapidly **in combat**. An ordinary campfire recharges over an unspecified longer duration, not instantaneously. **Previous ×1.5 ice vulnerability superseded**; retain **×3 Fire resistance** and high thermal inertia without inventing an ice-resistance multiplier. Exact heat capacity, fire effect magnitude, gain and expenditure rates remain open. Next material: Thornspring.



## November 8, 2026 — Hearthiron thermal exhaustion and hazardous heat discharge APPROVED; ordinary ice weakness superseded (LATEST AUTHOR REVISION)

**LATEST AUTHOR DIRECTION REPLACES THE PREVIOUS ELEMENTAL-DRAWBACK PROPOSAL:** Preserve Hearthiron's approved **×3 Fire resistance**, high thermal inertia, substantial Bellcopper-like weight and Reprise Bronze-like physical AR. **Retire the blanket ×1.5 ICE VULNERABILITY** from the previous Hearthiron proposal; it is **SUPERSEDED** by the author's preference for heat-resource exhaustion and hazardous heat release as meaningful drawbacks. This does **not** create any new numeric ice resistance or immunity; ordinary ice-magic damage still uses applicable rules and temperature-change resistance is a separate material property. The old ice multiplier appears in historical sections below solely for archival context.

**APPROVED HEAT AS FINITE RESOURCE:** Hearthiron stores heat and consumes that store primarily when the wearer deliberately uses its most active thermal abilities (notably Heavy's offensive furnace/contact heat and extended strong radiation, with intensity governing expenditure). At low passive warmth output, its exceptionally high storage retains heat over long periods; it does NOT lose combat-scale charges merely by existing. **Thermal exhaustion** reduces/disables heat-powered active abilities until replenished, without removing basic physical AR or inherently changing its approved baseline elemental Fire resistance. High-output heat use is a tactical choice with an actual opportunity cost, not unlimited free continuous fire damage.

**APPROVED HAZARDOUS LEAKAGE:** The wearer can regulate warmth and use proper insulation, but deliberate high-heat output can endanger nearby friends, carried gear, flammable environments, and creatures in direct contact. Use existing relevant fire/elemental injury, status, reach and action rules once fully specified; no automatically free attack or guaranteed burning through defenses. Consequences of exposing others to the heat are part of the material's downside.

**APPROVED DYNAMIC REHEATING, REJECTS SLOW-FORCED-RECHARGE DRAWBACK:** Extremely powerful external heat or suitably potent pyromancy can rapidly replenish Hearthiron **even during battle**, and opponents' sufficiently powerful fire effects may be exploited to refill it. **Ordinary campfires do not instantly restore useful combat reserves**; they replenish slowly over a deliberately **unspecified longer period**, with no arbitrary numeric hours or recharge turns canonized. Refilling depends on external intensity/actual available heat, not free magical MP generation from armor. Incoming fire remains a threat/effect according to the normal resistance framework; fuel recovery is not automatic universal immunity or unlimited stored capacity. The author rejected 'slow temperature adjustment' as a universal gameplay drawback, while high thermal inertia remains an inherent material property and intense heat explicitly overcomes practical recharge delays.

**UNRESOLVED (DO NOT INVENT NUMBERS):** heat reservoir capacity and units, active-ability costs, heat gain per spell/attack/ambient source, exactly when active fire damage applies, range, how fast extreme heat recharges, ordinary campfire time, interaction of generated heat with spell mana, whether capacity changes with quality/class, AR I–IV, MR, STR/SPD and fire/ice formula implementation. Resume pure-material design with **Thornspring** once the author is satisfied; no change to other materials.
## Hearthiron heat-bank armor approved; next Thornspring — November 8, 2026 (LATEST)

**APPROVED:** Hearthiron comparable in mass to Bellcopper (significantly heavy, even Light); physical AR comparable to Reprise Bronze (working QV Light30/Medium60/Heavy100); **×3 Fire resistance, ×1.5 ice vulnerability**; strong thermal inertia and lasting heat storage, without limitless energy. Light excellent personal cold protection, Medium radiates warmth to companions, Heavy controllable furnace/contact-burn offensive potential with proper insulation. Cold-weather defense and acute ice-magic weakness coexist. **Unapproved:** numerical heat depletion, contact damage, MR, exact STR/SPD; 'Bellcopper STR48' remains a proposal. Next pure material **Thornspring**. See Smithcraft/Equipment/Changelog.



## November 8, 2026 — Hearthiron weighted thermal armor and elemental profile APPROVED (LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL)

**HEARTHIRON AUTHOR-APPROVED MATERIAL/ARMOR IDENTITY:** Pure Hearthiron armor has **roughly the mass/burden of Bellcopper** at corresponding armor class, quality and coverage (both are unusually heavy, including Light class); its normal **physical AR should be on par with Reprise Bronze** by comparable quality/class. Use already-approved **Quality V Reprise Bronze** AR reference by protective class **Light30, Medium60, Heavy100** as the **working corresponding Hearthiron physical AR calibration**; this is not approval of a comprehensive Quality I–IV Hearthiron ladder or the still-open exact STR/SPD burdens. Its unusually high thermal inertia means it is **difficult to change the metal's temperature** in either direction: Hearthiron stores hot temperatures, gives off light/heat over years at modest demand, and can retain useful thermal conditions under harsh climates. It is NOT an infinite energy source; high heat output draws down stored heat faster. Standard proper armor insulation protects the wearer from the metal itself.

**AUTHOR APPROVES ELEMENTAL RELATIONSHIPS:** Hearthiron offers **×3 Fire RESISTANCE** and **×1.5 ICE VULNERABILITY**. Keep these distinct from ordinary AR/MR and physical armor penetration. The appropriate exact Elemental Magnitude/resistance formula placement remains unresolved; do NOT interpret ×3 fire resistance as '3× fire damage' or as a 3× AR multiplier, nor treat the ice vulnerability as invalidating its strong ordinary cold-weather insulation. Environmental cold preservation and acute magical ice vulnerability coexist as specified: magical/elemental ice is the weakness; harsh weather warmth is its specialty.

**AUTHOR APPROVES PRIOR ASSISTANT HEARTHIRON ARMOR CLASS DIRECTIONS:** **Light** armor supplies wearer comfort, persistent warmth and exceptional cold-weather survival. **Medium** can regulate and radiate heat to assist nearby companions. **Heavy** provides a more substantial stored-heat bank capable of **controllable dangerous radiant heat and contact burns in grapples/close-combat**, with built-in safe insulation for the wearer, not an arbitrary free attack action. User also approves the idea of controllable heat output including passive insulated/protective, radiant warming and high-output furnace-style applications; these are **design directions**, NOT new spell/status damage or exact activation actions. Any contact-burn/radiant injury must pass through appropriate existing fire/elemental combat rules; do not infer free harm through all physical protection, auto-ignite everything, or grant new attacks outside normal action economy.

**OPEN NUMERIC/PROCEDURAL FOLLOW-UP:** Precise heat storage units, warm/furnace active duration, rate of cooling and recharge, whether and how rapidly the wearer can toggle output, radiance distance and environmental counterplay, Light/Medium/Heavy quality I–IV AR, Hearthiron MR, detailed STR/SPD support and armor handling load, exact placement of fire/ice factors in future Elemental Magnitude formulas. Bellcopper's working burden STR48/−25% at QV Heavy remains a PROPOSAL, so no numerical Hearthiron burden is finalized by the author's 'about as heavy' approval. **NEXT PURE MATERIAL: THORNSPRING**, known for extraordinary elasticity, stored spring force and recoil management (risk: uncontrolled release). Its possible armor applications require proposal/approval rather than assuming existing mechanics.
## Bellcopper charges only on a real post-defense wound — November 8, 2026 (LATEST)

**AUTHOR APPROVED:** Heavy empty reservoir ×2 final physical Defense, Medium ×1.5; AFTER calculating the modified defense and resolving the hit, **only a real wound** (Solid or worse as applicable) triggers reservoir charge. Superficial/no-wound hit keeps it empty and protective, even if unmodified normal Defense would have produced a wound. Supersedes earlier 'would wound without bonus' trigger proposal. Once charged three uninterrupted turns to reset, subject to additional meaningful hits delaying reset. Next pure material **Hearthiron**, a finite red-hot heat reservoir lasting years under light use. See Changelog, Equipment and Combat.



## November 8, 2026 — Bellcopper reservoir charges ONLY on actual wounds after enhanced Defense (LATEST AUTHOR CORRECTION)

**HIGHEST-PRECEDENCE AUTHOR DECISION SUPERSEDES PRIOR TRIGGER PROPOSAL:** For Heavy Bellcopper (×2.00 final physical Defense Power when empty) and Medium Bellcopper (×1.50 when empty), resolve the relevant **armor-contacting physical attack WITH Bellcopper's enhanced final Defense Power** and all ordinary applicable combat rules. The reservoir begins charging / ceases to be Ready **ONLY IF the attack STILL actually wounds the wearer** (normally Solid-or-worse physical injury after applying the bonus). An intercepted attack that resolves **Superficial and inflicts no wound** DOES NOT charge it, even if that attack would have been Solid-or-worse against unboosted ordinary Defense Power. This explicitly REJECTS the earlier Quill proposal to use *would wound against normal Defense* as its activation criterion. It preserves the reward for successfully neutralizing blows rather than taxing every dangerous-looking hit.

**THREE-TURN RESET RULE:** Once an actual post-defense wound triggers reservoir charging, apply normal, unboosted physical Defense Power to later attacks until the reservoir has drained through three uninterrupted qualifying quiet turns. Meaningful additional armor-contacting impacts can prolong/reset its settling as previously directed, but do not reinterpret a successfully harmless *first* intercepted blow as stored energy. Exact later-hit 'meaningful' eligibility and turn-count bookkeeping can be specified when needed without contradicting the wound-only initial trigger. Keep armor AR equal to Boughsteel, Bellcopper considerably heavier, Light stealth hush/grapple vocal silence and Medium partial hush. The kinetic sound release's quantitative offensive effects, and specific STR/SPD values, remain OPEN. Example with END139 and AR115: normal Defense 32,546; ready Heavy 65,092; attack Power approx. 34,971 resolves superficial at 65,092, therefore **reservoir remains empty/ready**, not Ringing.

**NEXT PURE MATERIAL FOR DESIGN: HEARTHIRON.** Canon Hearthiron can retain red heat and emit light for years under low draw, cools faster under heavier heat demand and needs insulation to avoid burning people or surroundings. It is a heat battery, not infinite energy. Proposed armor direction is a temperature-management utility suit with user-selectable insulation or radiant warmth, without inventing specific fire damage/status/AR/MR/energy rates before the author's approval.
## Bellcopper AR equal to Boughsteel, Heavy +100% final Defense and Medium +50% when empty — November 8, 2026 (LATEST)

**APPROVED:** Bellcopper matches Boughsteel's physical AR by matching class/quality (QI–V Full Heavy 75/85/95/105/115), but is significantly heavier even in Light. Heavy Bellcopper with EMPTY kinetic reservoir has **×2 final physical Defense Power**; Medium has **×1.5 final physical Defense Power**, after regular END² + effective AR² calculation, NOT by multiplying inputs before squaring. Any stored kinetic energy restores normal Defense; three uninterrupted turns free of meaningful additional physical impacts empty/reset the reservoir. Prior proposal to halve Attack Power is superseded. **Open:** exact 'meaningful hit' trigger, reactive reservoir timing, sound effects, weight/STR/SPD numerics. Working weight suggestion QV Heavy STR48, SPD−25% is NOT approved. See Changelog/Smithcraft/Equipment/Combat.



## November 8, 2026 — Bellcopper Boughsteel-equivalent AR, greater mass, final Defense Power resonance (LATEST AUTHOR DECISION)

**LATEST APPROVAL:** At identical Quality, protective Light/Medium/Heavy class and actual coverage, pure Bellcopper has **the same physical AR as Boughsteel**, rather than a higher normal passive AR. Thus the established working Quality I–V **Full Heavy AR ladder is 75/85/95/105/115**. Bellcopper is **significantly heavier and demands more handling STR / has a worse intrinsic mobility load than Boughsteel at equivalent class/quality/coverage**, INCLUDING in its Light configuration. This supersedes any notion that Light Bellcopper must be physically lightweight merely because its protective class is Light. **Exact numeric weight/STR/SPD ladders remain to be author-approved**, including this author's yet-unprovided preference on how large 'significant' is. Low/medium/full class armor retains its own acoustic specialty from previous approvals.

**AUTHOR-SELECTED KINETIC RESONANCE MECHANISM supersedes earlier 'halve incoming Attack Power' assistant proposal:** When Heavy Bellcopper's kinetic reservoir is fully **EMPTY/Ready**, grant **+100% to final physical Defense Power** (= ×2.00). When Medium Bellcopper's reservoir is fully EMPTY/Ready, grant **+50% to final physical Defense Power** (= ×1.50). Calculate conventional physical Defense Power (effective END² + applicable effective AR², with armor penetration/gap rules) FIRST, then multiply **the final Defense Power linearly just once** as an after-calculation factor; DO NOT double base AR or END before squaring, alter MR, or multiply the attacker's Power. With ANY stored kinetic energy, either class returns to **normal unmodified physical Defense Power**. The bonus is for defending against an actual relevant physical armor-contacting attack when the reservoir is fully empty; its absorption response generates/stores kinetic energy to be released acoustically. This does not create a passive global Defense bonus for unarmored/uncovered sites, psychic attacks or spells producing direct energy without physical impact, nor a free second physical Defense calculation.

**APPROVED THREE-TURN DRAIN PRINCIPLE:** A charged reservoir needs **three turns to empty and reset**; meaningful additional impacts stop it emptying until the wearer's armor has a three-turn break from meaningful hits. Author describes all stored-energy cases as full loss of the special Defense multiplier, not a proportional bonus while partly charged. Acoustic output, when and where sound pulses are released, and interactions with stealth/hearing remain open. Preserve Light Bellcopper's near-total movement quieting and grapple-contact vocal hush; Medium's partial suppression; Heavy's one-hit reservoir specialty. No shield-style durability meter or separate raw-energy accounting is necessary.

**DESIGN QUESTIONS/PROPOSALS NOT YET APPROVED:** The precise trigger defining a 'meaningful hit' and whether an ultimately superficial hit made superficial by the doubled Defense still charges the reservoir. Quill recommends testing 'actual gear-contacting physical strike which would have been at least Solid versus NORMAL Defense Power' to avoid tiny harmless taps resetting the armor indefinitely, but this is NOT yet approved. Quill recommends resolving the charged/ready status upon the eligible intercepted blow and counting recovery at end of the wearer's turn without qualifying additional strikes. Numeric Heavy STR48/inherent SPD−25% versus Boughsteel candidate STR36/−20%, Medium/Light proportionate heavier profiles, are TEST OPTIONS ONLY, not author approvals. The mechanism of spectral/acoustic release, sound AoE/status, physical hit classification, effect of damaged armor and timing of reset remain open. **Examples (not independent approval of any new Power rule):** against END139, QV Full Heavy Bellcopper AR115 gives Defense 19321+13225=32546; empty multiplier x2 yields 65092; a QV 34970.87 attack is Solid normally and Superficial on first interception.

## November 8, 2026 — Oathsilver numbers and oath-assisted attunement APPROVED; Bellcopper three-class sonic identity APPROVED

### Oathsilver latest explicit numeric and narrative approvals

**AUTHOR APPROVES PREVIOUS OATHSILVER PROPOSAL:** Quality I–V Oathsilver provides a **+3% / +6% / +9% / +12% / +15%** bonus, applied **once** to each qualifying *existing installed enchantment's numerical effect*, never a blanket multiplier to total combat Power/Defense or an automatic extra spell rank, action, socket, or status. The approved working **Quality V Full Heavy Oathsilver** defensive baseline is **AR75 / MR60**, before installed enchantment effects. Eligibility and rounding for edge-case nonnumeric effects remain to be defined during enchantment design. Existing crafter-determined enchantment slot count, equipment Soul Strain, and item requirements do not change. Reattunement remains important following changed ownership.

**AUTHOR ADDS APPROVED OATH-BASED ATTUNEMENT DESIGN:** Keeping a sincere personal oath, following through on meaningful commitments and persistent honesty **can speed Oathsilver's attunement**, providing quest/character-building opportunities. Do not hard-code a universal moral alignment system, automatic truth detection, a fixed time discount or a requirement to undertake an arbitrary quest to wear the armor: exact attunement time, credible oath adjudication, sincerity, character-specific vows, transfer rules and shortcuts remain OPEN. This is an affirmative quest design hook and an attunement acceleration principle, not mandatory religious devotion.

### Bellcopper author-approved qualitative armor identity

**APPROVED DISTINCT LIGHT/MEDIUM/HEAVY FUNCTIONS:** Bellcopper already has acoustic resonance properties. **Light Bellcopper** is optimal for stealth: almost silences the wearer's bodily/equipment movement and can **suppress a grappled target's audible cries/vocal output at actual grapple/contact distance** to facilitate stealth takedowns. This is a sound-muffling capability, not an automatic grapple success, free injury or universal prohibition on spellcasting/psychic thought; standard grapple/reach/action rules still matter. **Heavy Bellcopper** is a single-impact kinetic/acoustic defensive specialist, storing/catching kinetic force from a physical strike and **releasing it as sound**. Its main strength is facing **one meaningful physical blow**, not an unlimited barrier against many quick blows. A charged/saturated resonator takes **three turns free from meaningful refreshing physical hits** to fully empty/reset; additional meaningful physical blows **prevent its reservoir from emptying** and consequently keep its best single-blow response unavailable (or sharply reduced). **Medium Bellcopper** combines weaker Light sound muffling (reduces sound but does not eliminate it) and a smaller/weaker version of the Heavy kinetic reservoir. Preserve distinct roles; no extra action, automatic acoustic injury or universally granted MR against sound without further approval.

**MECHANICAL DESIGN STILL OPEN, NOT AUTHOR-APPROVED NUMERICS:** Define exactly how physical strikes enter and leave a reservoir, the first-hit Power/Defense interaction, sound release timing/range/area and statuses, loudness and audible counterplay, what counts as a 'meaningful hit' for reservoir upkeep, whether the wearer chooses audible output direction, the three-turn timing at beginning/end of turn, Light vocal silencing against covered/obstructed targets, MR/AR/STR/SPD and passive Soul Strain. **QUILL TEST PROPOSAL, NOT CANON:** Treat Heavy as starting Ready; on an actual covered physical strike that *would* result in a Solid-or-worse physical injury, once per fully reset reservoir, halve incoming physical Attack Power for the strike and release a distinct sound pulse. Mark the suit Ringing after this one interception. While Ringing, do not halve additional blows; at each of wearer's turn ends without any qualifying meaningful strike, progress a 1/3, 2/3, 3/3 settling count back to Ready, with a qualifying hit resetting settling to zero. Medium may reduce only 25% of incoming Power with the same three-turn settling, rather than becoming a perpetual reduced-hit formula. No free audible attack Power or stun, hearing injury, AoE suppression, automatic perception bypass or silence on allied characters is created by the concept alone. Physical AR and END still contribute under their existing rules; testing should prevent double mitigation and perma-free sound damage. Alternatively adjust the exact multipliers and trigger upon the author's feedback.

## November 8, 2026 — Oathsilver loyal enchanter armor identity (LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL)

**APPROVED MATERIAL DESIGN:** Develop **Oathsilver armor** as a combination of long-term personal attunement ('loyal armor') and enhanced effectiveness of the wearer's **existing installed enchantments** ('enchanter's armor'). Its amplifier identity is **generalist**: eligible numeric/enchantment outputs from any permitted magical family may benefit, instead of Oathsilver requiring an element- or school-specific installation. It uses the same **crafter-determined enchantment/socket capacity** as relevant ordinary equipment: Oathsilver does **not** itself create or remove enchantment slots, grant free learned skills, bypass equipment grades, or install enchantments. It does **not** create a free reduction to Soul Strain or automatically remove normal installed-rune/gem requirements. It has **LOW physical AR** and **LOW-TO-MODERATE innate magical MR**, appreciably below protection-specialists like Boughsteel/Adamantite on AR and Nullporcelain on MR, as balance targets; detailed class/quality numeric tiers remain for author's later approval. Preserve existing canon that long-term bonding supports stable enchantments and that ownership transfer **may require reattunement**; precise bonding times, owner-recognition, item-transfer cooldown, effectiveness on temporary borrowing and magical tampering remain unsettled.

**BALANCE/FORMULA DIRECTION, NOT YET APPROVED NUMERIC RULE:** Prefer a **single bounded percentage bonus applied once to each eligible enchantment's own established numeric effect** after selecting the enchantment, rather than multiplying total attack Power/Defense or all of the wearer's attributes, or creating a second generic quality multiplier. Non-numeric enchantments, durations, categorical immunity, additional attacks, targets, ranks, and magic school access do not automatically benefit unless specifically supported by future definitions. Existing Soul Strain, enchantment slots, crafting rank, MR/AR scope and all ordinary equipment prerequisites remain in force. Oathsilver's special material effect should be relevant to installed enchantments regardless of whether they are elemental or protective.

**QUILL'S NONCANON NUMERIC OPTIONS FOR NEXT REVIEW:** Propose one bounded amplification schedule by Quality I–V, **+3%/+6%/+9%/+12%/+15%** to a qualifying enchanted effect (e.g., 20 units of fire resistance becomes 23 at QV), with no direct free increase to generic physical AR/MR or base spell Power. A possible *Quality V Full Heavy Oathsilver* calibration point is **AR75 / MR60**, less physically protective than QV Boughsteel AR115 and less magically defensive than QV Nullporc MR170. **Neither this amplifier schedule nor AR/MR point is yet approved** by the user. Also unresolved: interactions with enchantment effects that reduce AR penetration, the ceiling/floor on integer outputs, active charges, status thresholds, percentage-vs-percentage-point changes, and special enchantments that are not numerically scalable. Do not resolve these prematurely or retroactively change unrelated equipment.

**NEXT MATERIAL AFTER AUTHOR REVIEWS OATHSILVER:** Bellcopper, with existing sound-resonant smithcraft niche, is a suitable next pure material.

## November 8, 2026 — Reprise Bronze adaptive armor approved (LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL)

**AUTHOR APPROVES THE THREE-CLASS REPRISE BRONZE ARMOR PROPOSAL AND REJECTS AN ENCHANTMENT-SLOT PENALTY:** Reprise Bronze is a shape-memory alloy whose prepared/engraved **armor** can switch seamlessly between **Light (compressed), Medium (balanced) and Heavy (expanded)** configurations **at the start of the wearer's turn, without consuming an action**. The chosen form stays until the next opportunity at the start of the wearer's turn; no switching for each separate attack or reflexive change after seeing an enemy attack. Its **physical weight genuinely decreases on compression** and increases upon expansion, beyond mundane metal rearrangement, but this is reversible item transformation, not a source of freely harvestable material or arbitrary reshaping. This is a deliberate fantasy property compatible with Reprise Bronze's existing prepared-configuration restriction and Smithcraft Memory Form, which does not now grant arbitrary forms. **Armor class changes but coverage extent does not**: Full remains Full, Standard remains Standard, Partial remains Partial. Quality, installed enchantments, associated equipment-derived stat bonuses and Soul Strain do not change just because armor changes form. No special lost enchantment socket or extra enchantment bonus is imposed by the material.

**APPROVED WORKING QUALITY V REPRISE BRONZE ARMOR BENCHMARK (corresponding full-outfit class profiles, compare same coverage):** Light **AR30, MR0, post-quality STR12, inherent SPD−5%**; Medium **AR60, MR0, STR22, SPD−11%**; Heavy **AR100, MR0, STR35, SPD−19%**. Thus working Quality V Full Heavy Boughsteel **AR115**, versus Reprise Bronze's **AR100**, a **15 AR-point** (15% relative to Reprise's AR100) protection difference. Do not describe this as a universal Boughsteel advantage at every quality/class until their other values are approved. Reprise Bronze's MR0 does not negate specific installed magical enhancements or situation-specific defenses; the material itself grants no ordinary general MR. Each selected configuration uses its own actual STR requirement and existing unified equipment-shortfall rules. Existing generic Partial/Standard/Full coverage burden modifiers are still applied as appropriate, without inventing separate coverage-dependent AR or enchanted slot counts.

**STILL OPEN:** Reprise Bronze armor Quality I–IV AR/MR/STR/SPD ladders, how quality and smith preparation affect the transition, non-armor Memory Form options, costs, unusual damage cases and any per-item form customization beyond the standard three armor classes. No new Skill Point requirement or extra MP payment for ordinary transformations was approved. Next individual material to develop is **Oathsilver**, already canonically a long-term attunement and stable-enchantment alloy with reattunement concerns on transfer; do not assume it grants free Soul Strain relief.

## November 8, 2026 — Skyvein winged flight armor approved in direction (LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL)

**AUTHOR EXPLICIT CORRECTION:** Heavy Skyvein's powered flight comes from **scaled-up integrated Windling-STYLE rapid-beating mechanical wings, hummingbird-like**, directly attached to the wearer’s armor. **Actual Windling constructs do not tow, carry, or escort the armor** and there are no independent Windling units, squad CP charges, Cogsong Command Loom requirements or Cogmarshal prerequisites simply to operate built-in flight wings. The flight system is a dedicated engineered propulsion/stabilization mechanism, not independent Cogmarshal-controlled clockwork. The Skyvein frame supplies negative effective weight while **inertia remains**; wings produce controlled thrust, steering and short hovering. **An equipped onboard Leywell supplies all powered-flight MP**, not the wearer's natural MP by default.

**USER APPROVES THE PREVIOUS ASSISTANT'S SKYVEIN WORKING PROPOSALS:** Quality V Light **AR15 / MR10 / +12% SPD**, Medium **AR25 / MR20 / +8% SPD**, Heavy **AR40 / MR30 / +4% SPD**, as working suit profiles. Skyvein armor provides passive controlled descent/feather-fall and low-gravity-like jumping without flying indefinitely. Intrinsic **+1 equipment Soul Strain per meaningful fitted suit**, independent of quality and distinct from separately installed enhancements, follows the established equipment Soul Strain rules; no direct strain is assessed per flight turn. The Heavy winged variant is an engineered add-on requiring suitable integrated wings and a Leywell, not automatic free flight with every Skyvein suit. **Three continuous powered-flight turns** is the approved initial short-sortie engineering target; propulsion does not grant extra attacks, extra actions or a universal additional SPD multiplier. Passive feather-fall remains available with a depleted Leywell if the Skyvein material/frame is intact. Ordinary negative-weight Skyvein without the wings does not confer powered flight. The Light/Medium suits maintain higher passive SPD while Heavy offers a flight-platform niche, with possible other specialized configurations left for future designs.

**PROPOSED, NOT YET AUTHOR-APPROVED POWER DRAW:** A preliminary **6 MP per turn** from the equipped Leywell, **18 MP for a fully powered three-turn sortie**, chosen as an illustrative mid-level-spell-scale operating expense, **not** a verified equivalence to an approved comprehensive spell-cost ladder. Determine flight speed, direction change, Leywell storage/refill, wing operating recovery/cooldown, noise, exposed engine damage, use in grapples, load capacity, minimum coverage and crafting requirements later. A pilot does not gain free autonomous Windlings, no blanket zero-inertia movement, and ordinary equipment slot/control precedent remains intact.

**NEXT PURE MATERIAL:** After Skyvein, proceed to **Reprise Bronze**, the approved engineered shape-memory alloy with prepared/engraved configurations rather than arbitrary unlimited shapeshifting. New armor-specific numeric properties require their own design and author approval. Glassweave's revised nearly identical Boughsteel burden candidate and exact MR are still open.

## November 8, 2026 — AR/MR equipment shorthand approved; Nullporc ratings remain proposals

**LATEST AUTHOR TERMINOLOGY APPROVAL:** On armor and equipment cards and in future corresponding defense formulas, **AR = Armor Resistance** (the established physical armor-protection number, formerly spelled out **Armor Rating**) and **MR = Magic Resistance** (the protective equipment's applicable magical-defense rating). **AR's NAME changes, not its existing approved numerical values, penetration rules, or physical formula.** Distinguish AR from MR and from the creature's innate/stat-based END, Psychic Resistance and other specific defenses. The new MR shorthand does NOT by itself settle a mathematical treatment of Elemental Magnitude, direct psychic attacks, magical statuses, magic Power, magic bypass, or MR stacking; these remain subject to explicit author approval. Conventional armor generally confers no general-purpose MR by physical AR alone, as approved. Nullporcelain is physically AR0 at all qualities/classes; its MR is exceptionally high in relevant magical contexts. Its partial/full coverage rules, psychic-specific helmet exception, energy-spell non-breakage, indirect physical force bypass, and intact/cracked/full versus Shattered/none remain as previously approved.

**CURRENT NONCANON CALIBRATION PROPOSAL, NOT APPROVED NUMERIC RULE:** Test Full Heavy Nullporc **MR I–V 90/105/120/140/170** by comparison with the approved working Adamantite physical AR Quality I–V numbers, with Quality V Light MR85 and Medium MR120 as initial class candidates, but **do not treat either these MR values or their exact mathematical effects as final canon**. Prior candidate Quality V Full Heavy Nullporc physical support STR26, inherent SPD −15% also remains a proposal within author-approved weight order (Trufula < Nullporc < Boughsteel). Exact shatter triggers and repair remain open. Pure ceramic gear with zero AR should not quietly inherit AR25 joint underpadding.
## Nullporcelain AR0, high magic protection, intermediate burden — November 8, 2026 (LATEST)

**APPROVED:** Pure Nullporc has **physical AR0 at every quality and armor class**, with no default Medium/Heavy built-in AR25 padding exception. Anti-magic efficacy is intended to rival **Adamantite's physical defensive significance**, not reuse literal AR170 as magical arithmetic. At matched class/quality/coverage, weight and STR/mobility burden lie **between Trufula and Boughsteel**. Exact Magic/Elemental Magnitude/Psychic Resistance figures and shatter triggers remain open. Future Psycraft mechanics must account for real material-based mental defense from Nullporc helmets without giving ordinary physical armor psychic defense. See owning equipment/Smithcraft files and changelog.

## Ceramic armor retains protection until shattered; individual-material-first workflow — November 8, 2026

**APPROVED:** Extend Nullporc's intact/cracked **100%** protective effect and Shattered **0% until repaired** to genuinely ceramic-style armor, without a new durability meter or wearer-healing repair. Material-specific shatter thresholds remain open; do not automatically classify tough Glassweave as ceramic. **WORKFLOW:** finish and test pure materials individually before optional advanced composites on their own reference page. Nullporcelain numerical design is next; Celestite remains outside standard quality. See `07_Centaurs_and_Smithcraft.md`, `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md` and `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.

## Nullporc protection until shattered — November 8, 2026 (LATEST)

**APPROVED:** Nullporc (Nullporcelain) grants **full protection while intact or lightly damaged/cracked**. **Shattered** equipment gives **none of its own protective benefits** until repaired, irrespective of healing the wearer. No proportional cracked-state reduction or separate shield-style durability meter. Exact shattering trigger, repair requirements and numerical protections remain open. See `07_Centaurs_and_Smithcraft.md`, `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md` and changelog.

## Ratified armor/elemental doctrine and Nullporcelain design brief — November 8, 2026 (LATEST)

**CANON PHILOSOPHY:** Ordinary physical AR does not automatically reduce Elemental Magnitude or direct psychic effects. Appropriate material resistance, barriers, conductivity/insulation and attack pathways control such protection. A magically thrown physical rock is still a physical projectile, not an automatically resisted pure spell. **AUTHOR'S NULLPORCELAIN TARGETS:** poor and breakable physical armor; strong anti-spell defense without energy-spell breakage; area magic generally engages worn protection whereas targeted exposure can bypass; independent Nullporcelain anti-psychic/fear helmet as deliberate simple mixed-equipment exception. Injury-linked armor cracking and exact numbers are **PROPOSALS**, not yet canon. See `07_Centaurs_and_Smithcraft.md`, `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, and changelog.

## Trufula/Boughsteel composite final AR110 and presentation — November 8, 2026 (LATEST)

**AUTHOR APPROVED:** QV Full Heavy Trufula/Boughsteel **AR110** (supersedes AR105), post-quality **STR28, −16% SPD**. Universal Partial/Standard/Full support and penalties remain **17/−10%, 23/−13%, 28/−16%** with uniform protected main-surface AR110. The Trufula/Adamantite reference remains AR135. Keep advanced **composites on a separate presentation page** from introductory material identities; narrow-role composites need not be equally powerful. QV high-end protection is justified by costly skill mastery and substantial wealth. Ordinary physical armor does not grant AR against direct psychic attacks, nor does it automatically reduce Elemental Magnitude by sheer physical AR; physically relevant material defenses may apply. Exact elemental reduction math remains open. See `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `07_Centaurs_and_Smithcraft.md`, changelog.

## Universal armor-coverage burden and uniform composite AR — November 8, 2026 (LATEST)

**AUTHOR DIRECTION:** Universal coverage multipliers for physical STR and SPD burden: **Partial 60%, Standard 80%, Full 100%** of the same Full outfit; ceil STR, round percentage-point SPD penalty half-up. A listed composite main-surface **AR applies uniformly to every actually covered main surface**, not itemized material-specific body panels. Uncovered AR0 and protected Medium/Heavy joint AR25 remain. QV Full Heavy Trufula/Boughsteel **AR105, STR28, −16% SPD** => Partial **17/−10%**, Standard **23/−13%**; Trufula/Adamantite **AR135, STR55, −21%** => Partial **33/−13%**, Standard **44/−17%**. These fixed benchmark constructions do not create a universal arbitrary material mixing formula. See `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `07_Centaurs_and_Smithcraft.md`, and changelog.

# MYSTARIA — Canon Library Index

## Kharak Guard Flow Perfect Pivot chosen; physically countering Sorcery — November 8, 2026

**LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL:** Choose **Perfect Pivot (option A)** for Guard Flow's final stance enhancement, enriched by **Master's Answer (F)** learning from specific observed enemy techniques. Not a paid Fangscript reversal, no Guard Combo spent merely for passive use, no universal added action or magic resistance. **Guard Flow anti-magic identity:** superior timing and positioning to evade actual projectiles/blast areas, use physically valid cover, interfere with casting that remains interruptible, and exploit real observed caster habits. This does not inherently reduce spell Power, Elemental Magnitude or magically counterspell. Stage direction: Read the Blade → Break the Pattern → Perfect Pivot; final timing/caps/rank mechanics unresolved. Illustrative Blastline Pivot, Broken Cadence, Spell's Blindside and Master's Answer are proposals, not finalized learned techniques. See `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`, `08_Other_Crafts.md`, and `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.


## Kharak Guard Flow revisions and approved Fangscript meditation — November 8, 2026

**LATEST:** Kharak native red elemental manifestations normally express fire; compatible Drakyn dragonmark elemental alterations are approved in direction, precise mark interface unresolved. **Guard Flow stage 2:** qualify tactical maneuvers to defeat genuine flank, offhand, suppression, burst-concentration, or long-reach advantages; no automatic exemption from established Pressure or ×1.35 final-Power concentrated burst. **Guard Flow third stance upgrade remains OPEN**; *Perfect Reversal* rejected as redundant with Fangscript; multiple tactical capstones suggested but none approved. **Fangscript meditation NOW APPROVED:** uninterrupted 6-second intervals, `1 + floor(effective INT/20)` prepared momentum per interval, 10-minute duration, capped by relevant Combo capacity, spent on Fangscript, no advance combat-stance passive effects or extra stacked full meter. Details and remaining uncertainties in `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md` and `08_Other_Crafts.md` and latest changelog.


## Kharak Warflow stances and prepared Fangscript — November 8, 2026

**LATEST AUTHOR DIRECTION:** **Attack Flow** and **Guard Flow** are **two separate skill trees**, each developing a Combo pool toward intended **3 untrained / 8 mastered**; **only one martial stance's benefits at a time**. Attack Flow stance evolution: elementally imbued strikes, elemental movement, then small flat Attack Fangscript discount. **Guard Flow tactical overhaul requested:** avoid primarily numerical DEX/wound resistance/reversal; candidate enemy Reads, repositioning/redirection and counters **not approved**. Meditation may prepare spendable Fangscript momentum in ~**6-second intervals**, +1 per 20 effective INT and lasting ~**10 minutes**; these **numerical details remain proposals** until approved, not a definitive purchase rule. See `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md` and `08_Other_Crafts.md`. Existing combat Pressure Points are unrelated to Attack/Guard Combo.


## Thornguard MP reserve and the new Martial Arts framework — November 8, 2026

**LATEST APPROVAL:** Thornguard MP = **2 × floor(effective END)**, no personal mana shaping. Ordinary full **equipment Soul Strain capped at half their natural doubled reservoir**, protecting capacity (not a perpetual current-MP minimum). Compatible Concordance and external transfer pathways still required. Thornguard and Moontouched can learn otherwise eligible genuinely passive/mana-free/external-powered crafts and Jestercraft, not ordinary self-Sorcery. **Arts** are the intended martial branch: Thornguard mana-free enduring martial play, Kharak supernatural combat. Kharak intended structure: **Warflow Attack/Guard combos + stat-scaling named Fangscript** with meaningful noncombat preparation and inefficient direct red-MP casting. This is **approved system direction, not numerical/purchasable Art canon**. Details: `01_World_and_Shimmer.md`, `08_Other_Crafts.md`, `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`, `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.


## Kharak playable stats and natural bite — November 8, 2026

**CANON:** Level-1 base **STR 22 / DEX 15 / END 20 / INT 17 / SPD 14**; permanent **STR +25%, INT +25%**, no SPD penalty; effective **27.5 / 15 / 20 / 21.25 / 14**, combined total **97.75**. Their **natural bite** is a normal STR-based **Piercing** melee attack with **Medium Reach**, **Low/Point Coverage** and single attack-rating multiplier equal to the **ordinary longsword's primary F** (numerical longsword F **not yet finalized**). No free extra attack, no arbitrary extra weapon/material bonuses. Details: `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`, `02_Races.md`, `01_World_and_Shimmer.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`. Other Kharak natural attacks, mana affinity and abilities remain open.


## Approved Kharak Master Visual Reference — November 8, 2026

**CANON ART APPROVED:** The author's selected **second generated Kharak character sheet** (*Kharak — Rexfolk of the Imperium*) is the definitive master appearance reference. White/ivory scales, gold Tattao, red eyes, muscular humanoid-arm reptilian anatomy, smooth tail, fitted Claspjaw, dark industrial military clothing, cape-like coat and massive sword characterize this *example*, not all Kharak coloration or outfits. Full resolution PNG is saved in persistent **Library** at `/Mystaria/Approved_Art/Mystaria_Kharak_Approved_Master_Reference_White_Gold.png`; **image file not yet present in GitHub repository**. See `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md` and `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md`. Exact Claspjaw hardware follows separately approved chained technical sheet.


## Claspjaw definitive reference: chained split-front cuff — November 8, 2026

**LATEST CANON:** The author chose the *original Claspjaw — Standard Kharak Collar* parchment technical artwork as the standard design: broad brushed gunmetal industrial cuff, squared front terminals with short visible gap, a **loose chain bridging them**, **two dog tags hanging from the chain midpoint**, and side-mounted asymmetrical toothed handcuff ratchet and keyed housing; yellow-gold industrial diagonal stripes. This **supersedes the earlier fully unobstructed open C-shaped/no-chain ruling** (see below, now historical only). See `25_Imperial_Collar_Culture_and_Restraints.md`, `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md`, `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md` and changelog. Exact mechanical function remains open.


## Claspjaw open C-shaped front (SUPERSEDED) — November 8, 2026

**CANON:** The Kharak Claspjaw has a truly **unobstructed C-shaped front gap**, never a complete ring or a split front bridged by chain/metal. Two identification dog tags hang freely in the center, supported from one side without joining the ends. Keep asymmetrical industrial handcuff-style ratchet. Security/retention engineering is still open. See `25_Imperial_Collar_Culture_and_Restraints.md`, `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`, and `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md`.


## Claspjaw and reptilian scale variation — November 8, 2026

**CANON:** **Claspjaw** is the Kharak industrial collar's universal name, replacing the proposed Ironjaw. Kharak commonly have natural red, blue and deep-green scales; Naga and Serpentari may have elaborate real-snake-like natural patterning. Preserve the original supplied Kharak image's anatomy and pose when making the master reference, with newer humanoid-arm decisions respected. See `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`, `25_Imperial_Collar_Culture_and_Restraints.md`, `02_Races.md`, and `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md`.


## Kharak industrial collar direction (HISTORICAL; name since approved) — November 8, 2026

**APPROVED FEATURES:** heavy asymmetric metal collar, visible handcuff-like interlocking closure, central gap, hanging dog-tag identification, optional spiked undercollar, no Kobold-style orb. Universal name and exact mechanical/ornamental details remain open; “Ironjaw” is only proposed. See `25_Imperial_Collar_Culture_and_Restraints.md` and `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`.

## Kharak Tattao and differentiated uniforms — November 8, 2026

**CANON:** Spell **Tattao** (supersedes Tatao). Gold/silver mouth teeth and sunglasses are fashionable. Kharak uniforms vary with Coil wealth, function and institutional strictness, from farmhands and nightlife security to disciplined regulars. Authorized light uniforms may reveal backs, male chests and female cleavage; fabric/armor motifs visually continue covered Tattao. See `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md` and `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md`.

## Kharak punk civilian fashion — November 8, 2026

**CANON:** Provocative Coil Street Samurai punk streetwear, especially asymmetric woven tops, fitted reinforced trousers, belts, visible Tattao, cape-worn leather jackets; open vests, open-back/halter variations, fur and tooth accents. Tail-friendly, anatomy-conscious, revealing scales are normal. See `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md` and `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md`. Imperial uniform details, collars and racial art still pending.

## Kharak casual and military fashion direction — November 7, 2026

**CANON DIRECTION:** Dense woven cloth, traditional fur accents, jackets worn cape-style; preference for urban Coil Street Samurai among three compatible influences. Peacetime monster-hunting and slave-regulating military police favor light armor; full war gear evokes Viking fur/teeth and restrained power-armor aesthetics. Thornguard contrast with traditional knight/rogue dress. Specific outfits and powered armor mechanics remain unapproved. See `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md` and `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md`.

## Kharak anatomy and industrial fashion direction — November 7, 2026

**CANON DIRECTION:** Kharak have powerful, dexterous humanoid arms, distinct from Thornguard; their Imperial look combines Kobold-enabled technological practicality with dark street-samurai/fantasy military styling and industrial melee weapons. Casual dress includes furs and leather jackets, especially for men. Exact fashion, materials, collars and master artwork await approval. See `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md` and `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md`.

## Kharak Hearth speech — November 7, 2026

**CANON:** Kharak may swear freely around hatchlings but soften their tone, conduct and patience; **“little fang”** is an affectionate nickname. See `12_Languages_and_Linguistic_Customs.md` and `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`. Fashion and military visual concepts remain under development.

## Kharak profane speech — November 7, 2026

**CANON:** “Fangrot!” is the everyday exclamation; “Arrest's Arse!” a blasphemous curse; “Rot your word!” a serious condemnation; and “Coward!” a commonplace accusation. Their colorful approved vulgar insults and contrast with tender speech toward hatchlings are in `12_Languages_and_Linguistic_Customs.md`. “Shit and splinters!” is rejected. Kharak fashion, collars, and three-quarter racial art are next in the approved production sequence.

## Kharak signature expressions — November 7, 2026

**CANON:** “Count them home.”; “My word stands.” / “Then I stand with it.”; and the revised curse “Rot your word.” Kharak vulgar profanity and everyday exclamation remain in development; “Cracked shell!” is not the selected staple. See `12_Languages_and_Linguistic_Customs.md` and `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`.

## Kharak development sequence and Vornak open history — November 7, 2026

**AUTHOR DIRECTION:** Keep Vornak's detailed personal history unresolved for future stories. Develop Kharak linguistic customs first, then casual/uniform fashion, collar style, and a three-quarter-view master racial illustration. New designs and phrases require approval. See `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`.

## Hatchbane evidentiary procedure and Deep Inquiry writ — November 7, 2026

**CANON:** Court of Fangs assesses motive/opportunity/means and authenticates **mirror evidence**; Third/Second/First Degree require preponderance, clear-and-convincing, and beyond-reasonable-doubt proof respectively. Five stages: petition/protection, investigation, hearing, judgment, sentence/review. Exceptional compelled mind/memory examination requires an independently authorized, narrow **Writ of Deep Inquiry**, substantial prior evidence, exhaustion of safer methods and proportionality; free persons retain separate silence and mental privacy rights. See `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`. Detailed appeals and slave safeguards remain open.

## Mirror evidence and mental privacy in Imperial courts — November 7, 2026

**CANON:** Mirrors capture sights and sounds, while Orbs store recordings; legal video/audio is called **mirror evidence**, not Orb recordings. Psionic mind reading, Orbcraft memory extraction and Tidekin Mirrorwake may support guilt or innocence but are fallible, and compelled deep probing is dangerous and exceptionally restricted in principle. Free persons have **independent rights to silence and mental privacy**; nonconsensual emotion reading cannot be used against them as evidence or grounds for suspicion. Masters exercise substantial authority over slaves' questioning. Detailed **Writ of Deep Inquiry** safeguards and Hatchbane evidence thresholds remain **proposals**, not canon. See `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md` and `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.

## Kharak protection of other races' children — November 7, 2026

**CANON:** Acts resembling Hatchbane against children of other species are broadly despised by Kharak as a matter of **informal honor**, without automatically constituting the same formal Kharak offense. This moral development contrasts with early Khans' Extinction campaigns; Vornak formalized a reform movement developing over generations, rather than inventing compassion alone. See `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`.

## Hatchbane degrees and Kharak historical Extinction — November 7, 2026

**CANON:** Third-Degree Hatchbane concerns substantial neglect and mistreatment with reparations and improvement; Second Degree concerns substantial injury/kidnapping and culpable failure to protect, ordinarily punished by the permanent painful **Banebrand**; First Degree includes deliberate killing, commissioned assassination and culpably foreseeable lethal kidnapping, with forfeiture of title/Coil or **Decimation**. Ancient adult-offspring life substitution survives only as a nearly unused non-Imperial, non-Arrest tribal blood-law exception; only a true Serpentari may rescind that ancient provision. Earlier Khans practiced **Extinction** of rival bloodlines and some minor species, including children; modern Kharak protection of hatchlings represents imperfect moral reform. Details and boundaries: `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`.

## Serpentari four-name meanings and inherited Ascendancy — November 7, 2026

**CANON:** Ancient true Serpentari have four proper names: **Self, Blood, Great Coil, Ascendancy**. The fourth name is **inherited at birth** and may later be replaced with an **earned** name celebrating demonstrated achievement; retention is permissible. Additional titles are separate. This does not alter the approved two-name Untouched Serpentari tradition. See `24_Racial_Naming_Conventions_and_Namebook.md`. The historical Serpentari First Sovereign who defeated Vornak is not yet named.


## Kharak Hatchbane crime terminology — November 7, 2026

**CANON:** The Kharak crime of harming an egg or hatchling is called **Hatchbane**; its perpetrators are **hatch-traitors**. This replaces the unapproved Hatchtreason terminology. Statutory elements and sentencing remain open. See `33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`.

## Ancient Serpentari four-name convention — November 7, 2026

**CANON FORMAT:** True ancient Imperial Serpentari possess **four proper names**, optionally plus earned ceremonial/regnal titles. Their sound blends aristocratic dynastic tradition with exotic grandiloquent titles. **PROPOSAL:** Personal Self, ancestral Blood, Great Coil, and Ascendancy as the four positions. This supplements rather than replaces the established **Untouched Serpentari** two-name namebook. Reconcile any 'High King' as appropriate with the established **Nine Thrones** and **First Sovereign**. Kharak *Hatchtreason* remains an unapproved crime-name proposal. See `24_Racial_Naming_Conventions_and_Namebook.md` and `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.


## Kharak families, Tattao, religious devotion and Vornak — November 7, 2026

**CANON:** The Kharak's **Hearth** and **Warclan** family organization, intense hatchling protection, **First Oath** at young warriorhood and **Second Oath** at army service, and the **Tattao** allegiance tattoos favoring Coil over Warclan imagery are recorded in [`33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`](33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md). Kharak children **do not inherit their parents' slave status**, unlike the usual Imperial maternal rule. Imperial Kharak prominently worship **Arrest**; wilder clans often favor **Baphomet**. Personal + Warclan + earned epithet naming is approved: **Kharzug Vornak, Khan of the Unbroken Word**, is conventionally quoted as **Vornak**; his descendants serve the senior Matriarch. The Khan's **Honored Defeat** by an unnamed Serpentari sovereign is approved. True Serpentari monarch naming, tattoo pattern specifics, collective hatchery-challenge legal standing and chronology remain open.


## Kharak supplemental approvals — November 7, 2026

**CANON:** The **Court of Fangs**, **Oathspeaker** advocates and **Crown Arbiter** judges, approved cross-cultural Kharak/Naga influences, **Khan of the Unbroken Word** epithet and three maxims, and a historical strategic contest preceding modern Golden Conquest are now recognized in [`33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`](33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md). INT 17 is a **preferred balance target, not a final numeric sheet**. Clan/family systems, full Khan name, opposing Serpentari king, decisive historic contest outcome and specific dress/language designs remain pending.


## Kharak (Rexfolk) martial culture — November 7, 2026

**CANON / DEVELOPMENT:** **Kharak** is the approved racial name for the Rexfolk, the Imperium's enormous tyrannosaur warriors. Their proud allegiance philosophy, stigma of clanless freedom, barbaric professional ranks (Fang through High Warchief), **First Fang** appointment, conquered-clan history, and First Fang's institutional Right of Challenge have been approved in principle. Their historical Serpentari restraint, later Naga military integration and favored Drakyn legal intermediaries are established; precise legal mechanisms, legendary Khan and early Imperial sports details, INT/STR numeric sheet, full red-mana/bite/weapon mechanics, fashion and linguistics await decisions. See [`33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md`](33_Kharak_Rexfolk_Culture_and_Command.md).


## Approved Duskpaw salvage fashion and Lastwear — November 6, 2026

**CANON:** [`32_Duskpaw_Fashion_and_Lastwear.md`](32_Duskpaw_Fashion_and_Lastwear.md) owns exceptionally common torn, mismatched, visibly repaired and repurposed **free-chosen Duskpaw clothing**, Cache garment-sharing and meaningful heirlooms, asymmetrical formalwear, reclaimed open-lock accessories, and **Lastwear** (visibly associated with previous wearer's death). Lastwear is used as salvage, memorial, or trophy dress without formal grading. Artificially distressed imitation is generally mocked for wastefulness rather than universally condemned. **Enslaved Duskpaws in the Scarlands and Imperium may be forced into maintained, regulated occupational uniforms**; specific cuts and role designs remain **PROPOSAL**, not approved. Nightlock remains unchanged.

## Approved Bloomkin Bannerweave hero-and-squire fashion — November 6, 2026

**CANON:** [`31_Bloomkin_Bannerweave_Fashion.md`](31_Bloomkin_Bannerweave_Fashion.md) defines **Bannerweave**, the Bloomkin-led complementary heraldic outfits of Thornguard knights and their Bloomkin Shieldmates; **Twinmantle** for especially coordinated pairs; the artistic milestone **First Fitting**; grand self-proclaimed heroic introductions, profound admiration for gods' and legendary heroes, and particular reverence for **purple**. Bloomkin tailor both the knight's cape and their own heroic squire outfits. Celebrated Thornguard legends attract Bloomkin eager to serve as Shieldmates. Existing Concordance: Thornguard provide white, Bloomkin shape it. The Oath of Protection and potential unequal legal status are not erased; no clothing mechanics added.


## Approved Moontouched warmth and maid fashion — November 6, 2026

**CANON:** [`30_Moontouched_Imperial_Fashion.md`](30_Moontouched_Imperial_Fashion.md) records Moontouched **casual sweaters and socks for warmth**, **pantyhose common in formal outfits**, and **maid uniforms specifically for maid employment or maid-themed hospitality**, not default everyday rabbitfolk clothing. Maid cafés and similar venues are popular Moontouched cultural/hospitality settings. Uniforms do not determine enslaved/free status. Distinctive ritual outfits, colors and detailed gift/accessory fashions remain proposals. Existing Moonlace collars and racial mechanics are unchanged.


## Tidekin stature and Neri voice update — November 6, 2026

**CANON:** Typical adult Tidekin stature is **around 4 feet**, using the approved Neri “Wrong-Way” Reedwake reference proportions as the visual standard. This replaces the earlier default 4–5-foot summary, without making all Tidekin exactly the same height. See `02_Races.md` and `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md`. In Slate's campaign, Neri calls Lillia **“Babe”** frequently but not constantly, occasionally uses surfer slang, and has a history of surfing; an owned surfboard is **possible, not confirmed**. See `campaigns/slate/Live_Chronicle.md`.

## Approved Tidekin fashion, swimwear and Kin-knots — November 6, 2026

**CANON:** [`29_Tidekin_Imperial_Fashion.md`](29_Tidekin_Imperial_Fashion.md) owns approved Imperial Tidekin wear: **close-fitting swimmer-friendly clothing, with bikini tops very common in casual wear**, removable decorative layers, aquatic practicality, Raft Kin-knots as sentimental accessories, colorful individualized free-person dress, and occupation-adapted Saltbound attire. Approved existing Harborband design (two separated corrosion-resistant rings; upper value, lower security/obedience) is unchanged; exact coding remains unresolved. This clothing direction creates no magical equipment effect or universal dress regulation.


## Approved Kobold fashion and eyesight — November 6, 2026

`27_Kobold_Imperial_Fashion.md` records the approved Kobold professional/casual style, including petticoats and professor/mechanic attire even for prestigious household specialists, common spectacles and naturally weak distance vision from cave adaptation (with exceptions such as Corvyn-imprinted Kobolds), and the haggard, scorched or torn clothes often preferred by independent free Kobold engineers. The exact effect of imprinting on acuity is unresolved. **Presentation Dress** (polished scholarly formality) and **Demonstration Dress** (practical work attire) are now approved cultural conventions, alongside the cherished Working Coat and symbolic/functional Proud Tool distinction. Neither is a mandatory uniform nor proof of competency. More elaborate Academic Gala or strict tool/patch etiquette remains **PROPOSAL**.

## Approved Naga fashion and ceremonial dress — November 6, 2026

**CANON:** [`26_Imperial_Fashion_and_Dress.md`](26_Imperial_Fashion_and_Dress.md) owns the approved Naga fashion dossier: short dancer-like silks, revealing but socially respectable silhouettes, conspicuous impractical jeweled chains/manacles reflecting historical Serpentari enslavement, and Matriarch circlets customary even for the poor (copper), but **not legal mandates**. Ceremonial attire invokes historical servitude more directly; literal self-binding is exceptional, notably zealot reenactments or abolitionist protests, never a universal rite. Political interpretations differ, including Unbound Daughters' criticism of current slaveholding. Existing Imperial collar mechanics and other peoples' fashion remain unchanged. See `10_Canon_Changelog.md`, `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md`, `18_The_Gilded_Coil_Imperial_Gazetteer.md`.

## Approved Coilsting Collar name — November 6, 2026

**Coilsting Collar** is the user-approved name of the specialized Imperial magical restraint previously described by the working title **Shock Collar**. **Censure Collar / Censureband** was a naming proposal and is not canon. All established behavior and unresolved numerical details remain unchanged. The owning source is `25_Imperial_Collar_Culture_and_Restraints.md`; revision status is in `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.

## Approved slavery philosophies, Imperial collar culture and Orb interfaces — November 6, 2026

**Player-facing world principle:** Naga enslave for **order/hierarchy**, Thornguard for **enforced protection of the weak**, and Vren for **shameless exploitation and increased personal power**. Start with `01_World_and_Shimmer.md`; the new owning reference `25_Imperial_Collar_Culture_and_Restraints.md` consolidates the approved visual/racial collar decisions, the Duskpaw **open-lock emancipation** symbol, Tidekin two-ring Harborband classification, Kobold direct Cipherlight Orb interfaces, and the three specialized expensive Imperial magical collars. **Coilsting Collar** is the approved name of the previously working-titled Shock Collar; **Censure Collar / Censureband** is not approved. Magical collar numerical mechanics remain unresolved. The Imperial user-approved cultural defaults are mixed public/official identification, class-dependent ornament, and limited ceremonial/security silencing. See `08_Other_Crafts.md`, `18_The_Gilded_Coil_Imperial_Gazetteer.md`, `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md`, and `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.


## Approved racial naming conventions and 450-name library — November 5, 2026

**CANON:** [`24_Racial_Naming_Conventions_and_Namebook.md`](24_Racial_Naming_Conventions_and_Namebook.md) is the controlling reference for approved naming conventions and **30 common-name examples each for 15 groups (450 total)**: Naga, Kobolds, Moontouched, Bloomkin, Thornguard, Tidekin, Duskpaws, Vren, Corvyn, Driders, Untouched Serpentari, Drakyn, Centaurs, Firstshaped and Nymbrin. Naga inherit maternal syllables **flexibly**; Kobolds retain birth names before First Devotion; Corvyn retain birth names before Ink Names. Names are reusable cultural examples, not 450 established NPCs. Follow the owning dossier for rules on lineage, titles, earned names and Firstshaped parent-race identity. `12_Languages_and_Linguistic_Customs.md` governs speech and language; `02_Races.md` governs racial identity.


**Library edition:** November 4, 2026 — approved Warlock, Gilded Coil, Mnemonic Arts and repository-audit reconciliation  
**Purpose:** A stable, modular reference for the Mystaria Lore project, so multiple conversations can retrieve approved mechanics without relying on incomplete chat memory.  
**Scope:** Lore, divine history, Shimmer, Sorcery, Warlocks, Jestercraft, Drakyn, Centaurs, equipment, racial Crafts, approved linguistics, and visual references.

## Approved Hero Gem wealth, resale and threat rewards — November 5, 2026

`09_Equipment_and_Economy.md` owns the **approved typical-Hero Gem progression**: target equipment purchase-value `W(L) = 3 + 3997 × ((L−1)/99)^2.3` for levels 1–100, from **3 Gems at level 1** to **4,000 Gems in currently owned equipment at level 100**, with a typical Hero replacing their equipment at each quality tier. **30% of lifetime Gem earnings** is the typical consumable allocation; Gem Shops buy back **their previously sold items at 50% of original price**. The reference full-replacement milestones are levels 10/25/50/75/100; expected resale losses total **~1,511 Gems**, yielding **~7,872 lifetime Gems earned** and **~2,362 spent on consumables** by level 100. **Gem threat modifiers match the unchanged canonical XP modifiers**: Horde ×0.1, Minion ×0.4, Henchman ×1, Elite ×2; Bosses receive a special **two-level Gem-budget award** (without changing the existing two-level XP rule). Reference calculations, limits, and open payout rounding details are in the owning file. This is a typical economic target, not an automatic Gem award or item-use level gate.

## Historical Shimmer XP and encounter threat — November 5, 2026 (Boss XP superseded November 20)

> This section preserves the November 5 historical approval. Its former **two-level Boss XP** claim is **SUPERSEDED** by the November 20 one-level ruling at the top of this index and in `01_World_and_Shimmer.md`; other still-applicable ordinary XP parameters remain effective.

`01_World_and_Shimmer.md` owns the newly approved **XP to next level = L × (L + 1)** curve, where **L is the current Hero level**. Qualifying non-Boss enemy or equivalent patron-approved quest XP is **2 × encounter level × threat modifier**, rounded to whole XP (minimum 1 XP for a qualifying award). **Horde ×0.1** (suggested enemy level ≤50% of Hero), **Minion ×0.4** (>50–80%), **Henchman ×1** (>80–110%), **Elite ×2** (>110–140%), and **Boss** (usually >140%, or specially designated) awards exactly **two full level advances** from the Hero's current XP progress instead of the ordinary formula. The GM assigns actual threat roles based on encounter capability; relative level bands are guidance. A level-1 Hero needs 2 XP, usually one equal-level Henchman; level 100 needs 10,100 XP, approximately **51 equal-level Henchmen**. Existing patron XP qualification, mundane quest limitations, stat growth and SP costs remain unchanged. No numeric anti-farming penalty or party split is approved.

## Approved Drider anatomy revision — November 4, 2026

Driders have dark skin reminiscent of a drow elf, pointed ears, **two arms**, and **six long spider-like legs** supporting an enormous arachnid lower body. They have **four solid-color eyes with no pupils**; eye color varies between individuals. Their long legs and imposing bodies make them **one of Mystaria's largest mortal races**. `02_Races.md` owns the revised description; `01_World_and_Shimmer.md` removes the obsolete Four-Arm Coordination benefit, and `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md` records the illustration requirements. Earlier four-armed Drider references are superseded. This ruling does not revise Arrest's own anatomy or establish numerical racial stat changes.

## Approved official-art collar convention — November 4, 2026

In official Mystaria art, slave races should **usually wear visible collars**, with exceptions appropriate to the character, narrative context or explicit art direction. `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md` owns this presentation default; it does not make every individual enslaved or change racial mechanics.

## Current official art direction — November 5, 2026

Direction 4, detailed ink-and-color fantasy illustration, is the defining official style; this corrects the mistaken Direction 5 selection. `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md` records the full treatment and durable links to Race Image Origin (v6) and its Direction 4 style example (v10). Race reference sheets now take priority; the old website queue and integration are deferred. Adult female busty/voluptuous appearance remains operative across all races.

## Earlier global official-art style and adult female figures — November 4, 2026 (rendering superseded)

`11_Visual_Style_Guide.md` now requires the author's seven graphic animated style attributes for all official Mystaria art going forward: simplified strong shapes, clean varied bold contours, expressive stylized proportions, two or three hard cel-shading values, limited separated colors and colored shadows, selective readable detail, and dynamic expressive gestures. This supersedes the painterly living-storybook rendering requirement. `02_Races.md` records adult females of all Mystaria races as characteristically busty and voluptuous, broadening the earlier Corvyn-specific guidance while preserving each race's established anatomy and form. Individual image approval remains separate.

## Read this before adjudicating a rule

**Canon precedence:**

1. The user's **latest explicit approval or correction** governs the subject, even when an older master compendium or generated guide disagrees.
2. A dedicated approved canonical source (e.g. Warlock rank manuscript or revised Jester Craft) controls its own details over short summaries.
3. The November 1 `Mystaria_Canon_Compendium.md` supplies background canon **only insofar as no later decision contradicts it**.
4. An assistant's unapproved proposal or recommended formula is **not canon**, even if fully developed or contained in this library.
5. Missing exact mechanics, absent named ranks and unresolved count conflicts must be labeled **UNKNOWN/UNRESOLVED**; never infer them just to make the documents look complete.

**Status key:** **CANON** = explicitly approved and operative; **CANON WITH SOURCE GAPS** = approved but some exact recorded details inaccessible; **UNDER REVISION** = older approved content flagged for potential replacement; **PROPOSAL** = presented, not canon.
