The GitHub library now includes the restored approved `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md` and `15_Naga_Psycraft_Canon.md`. Operative Naga terminology is **Psycraft / Psychics**; the Grand Coil Games rotate among **major Imperial cities**, and **Sathryx is the sole capital**. Firstshaped rates are approximately **10% of Centaurs (Ponies)** and **5% of Drakyn**, overriding generic rarity for those races. The earlier Red `Ignis` seven-reported/six-named alteration discrepancy was **resolved by the author's explicit removal of the unidentified seventh purchase**; definitive selected Blue/Green/White alterations and the Warlock END threshold formula remain unresolved. Kobold starting statistics and racial modifiers have **not** been approved.

## Approved Mindprism, Echo Economy and Skillprints — November 4, 2026

`19_The_Mnemonic_Arts_and_Echo_Economy.md` controls the **approved Mnemonic Arts** of retinal **Mindprism**: **Echoing** consumes extravagant mana and is ordinarily impractical; **Mindcut** rapidly removes the source's original memories and can cause permanent brain damage; neurological strain from either cannot be healed by magic. An **Echo** remains bound to its original owner, cannot be destroyed by mortal technology, and returns if not held by a maintained, mana-consuming **Everseal** while that owner is alive; **the owner's death ends automatic return**. **Skillprints** can grant **actual Shimmer skills counted as purchased for 0 SP**, subject to compatibility, normal prerequisites and level gates; **about 5 SP worth per month** is the modest safe assimilation rate. They are very rare medium/high-level Hero rewards. **Dreamcasting** is real-time experiential entertainment. **Echo Vaults**, **Memory Bonds**, **Living Reliquaries** and **Hollow Reliquaries** are established. `18_The_Gilded_Coil_Imperial_Gazetteer.md` and `01_World_and_Shimmer.md` include corresponding cross-references; `12_Languages_and_Linguistic_Customs.md` records technical terms. Exact MP costs, return timing and exceptional procedures remain unresolved.

## Approved Cipherlight / Rainbow network expansion — November 4, 2026

`18_The_Gilded_Coil_Imperial_Gazetteer.md` establishes **Cipherlight**, the hazardous encoded light transmitting Orbcraft/Mirrorcraft messages through lighthouse relays and reflective underwater pipelines. An active network's atmospheric coverage is its **Rainbow**, a recognizable, usually subtle aurora-like display that intensifies with heavy traffic. Even a small Coilnet outside established cities, such as at a Drakyn lair, can attract attention. Unexpected Rainbows can alert security to possible espionage or foreign influence, without identifying or proving criminal activity. Regional interpretations are summarized in that dossier. `12_Languages_and_Linguistic_Customs.md` includes the **approved Kobold and regional Cipherlight/Rainbow sayings**. `13_Wakecraft_The_Six_Arts_of_the_Living_Tide.md` documents the **approved Tidekin Mirrorwake interaction**: exceptionally detectable Cipherlight passages, strong water impressions, potential overload/erasure-by-obscuring of other evidence, and sophisticated false impressions. Exact forensic thresholds, beam-coding and opposition mechanics remain unresolved. No existing Wakecraft ranks or SP costs change.


## Approved Gilded Coil imperial gazetteer — November 4, 2026

The user approved the complete *Gilded Coil — Heartland of the Naga Empire* manuscript where it does not conflict with later canon. The authoritative consolidated regional dossier is [`18_The_Gilded_Coil_Imperial_Gazetteer.md`](18_The_Gilded_Coil_Imperial_Gazetteer.md). It establishes the **Inner Gardens / Outer Reaches / Silent Works** landscapes, four principal cities (**Sathryx**, **Velmora**, **Serrath Quay**, **Kheth-Vael**), Worldspindles/Coilworks, Imperial governance, Scale Register, **Scale Guard / Mirrorwardens / Coil Tribunals / Spindlewatch**, and regional economy, people, ecology and adventuring relevance. **Sathryx is the one imperial capital**. The four-yearly **Grand Coil Games rotate among major Imperial cities**. Enslaved people form **a majority of central-city residents**, with **no approved percentage** (the old 50–65% estimate is superseded). Subject-specific racial, magical and sporting dossiers govern their own mechanics.

## Approved raw blue mana effect against wounded targets — November 4, 2026

`04_Warlock_Manacraft.md` owns the approved raw-blue projection clarification: **sensory overload and amplification of existing wound pain**, with secondary **fractured bodily perception and disrupted coordination**. This is distinct from structured Sorcery and does **not** freeze the target or become a splash of water. Approval covers this blue effect only; other optional proposals remain unapproved. Existing Warlock skills, costs, prerequisites, handling limits and **Blue Hole — Shifting Depth** are unchanged. Exact duration, numerical penalties, resistance procedure and damage formula remain **UNRESOLVED**.

## Revised Warlock Manacraft approved — November 4, 2026

`04_Warlock_Manacraft.md` now controls the **recovered replacement five-tree system**: **Mana Reach** (Unseen Hand → Ley Carving); **Mana Power** (Focused Expression → **Mana Singularity**, including **Green Hole — Genesis Bloom**); **Mana Harvest** (Afterglow → Soul Reaping); **Mana Imbuement** (Reservoir Seal → Soulwork); **Mana Dominion** (Deflection → Null Dominion). All **25 ranks cost 5 SP each (125 SP mastery)** at ordinary level gates; innate Manabending is free. **Basic range is `2 + 2 × effective INT` meters**; approved temporary capacity in **one hand** is **`max(1, floor(natural effective END / 5)) MP`** (20% of natural effective END, rounded down, minimum 1 MP). The former 1–3 spell-load scheme is superseded; safe holding remains 30 seconds, absolute maximum 60 seconds. All **25 approved new MP activation/upkeep schedules** are recorded in the owning dossier. The old 25 ranks and their MP fees are **SUPERSEDED** wherever different, not optional parallel abilities; proposed progressive SP costs are rejected. The old END spell-load boundaries are **RESOLVED** by direct MP Hand Capacity; same-action activation payments and some high-level scale/duration limits remain **UNRESOLVED**.

## Approved Imperial sports and Great Coil Circuit — November 4, 2026

The user's explicit approval canonizes the entirety of the recovered *Games of the Gilded Coil* manuscript. Read [`17_Imperial_Sports_and_Great_Coil_Circuit.md`](17_Imperial_Sports_and_Great_Coil_Circuit.md) for the six competitions (**Coilrun**, **Worldspindle Trials**, **Golden Conquest**, **Thousand Locks Regatta**, **Hollow Crown Expeditions**, **Starwarden Pursuit**), the **Great Coil Circuit**, Imperial sporting calendar, **Grand Coil Games**, professional and enslaved champions, sponsor politics, manatech broadcasting, and adventure hooks. The manuscript supplies setting objectives, examples and some typical competition structures, but does **not** establish a complete tabletop match-resolution system or numerical rules for every sport. The approved Naga magical tradition is **Psycraft** and practitioners are **Psychics**.

## Approved Naga Psycraft — November 4, 2026

**Psycraft** is the approved name of the Naga's predominantly blue-mana psychic tradition; **Crowncraft** is the superseded name. Practitioners are called **Psychics** (singular **Psychic**); **Crownmind** is a superseded, noncanonical title. Established proper names such as **The Perfect Crown** remain unchanged. `15_Naga_Psycraft_Canon.md` is the authoritative full reference: innate **Sovereign Sense**, plus **Psychokinesis**, **Telepathy**, and **Divination**, each with five sequential ranks at **5 SP each** (**75 SP** total). The approved manuscript also establishes the three academies, manatech connections, institutional roles, limitations and culture. Earlier `08_Other_Crafts.md` Crowncraft gap is resolved by this dossier; exact per-technique MP costs are not specified.

## Approved Tidekin and Vren racial Crafts — November 4, 2026

**Tidekin:** `13_Wakecraft_The_Six_Arts_of_the_Living_Tide.md` supersedes the old three-technique Wakecraft summary; innate Hydrokinesis, six five-rank skills and 150 SP full mastery.

**Vren:** `14_Vren_Sanguine_Arts_Canon.md` contains Blood Boon and the six Disciplines of the Night; revised Blood Magic IV, Pulse Echo, Beastheart III–V and Celerity III supersede their original versions. Blood Boon activation follows `ceil(Hero Level / 25)` MP; level 25 costs 1 MP, level 26 costs 2 MP. The former table conflict is resolved by the November 4 clarification. Each rank costs 5 SP; 150 SP full mastery.

`08_Other_Crafts.md` remains an older overview for these two races and is subordinate to the approved dedicated files.

## Approved linguistic canon — November 4, 2026

Read [`12_Languages_and_Linguistic_Customs.md`](12_Languages_and_Linguistic_Customs.md) for approved regional languages, cultural speech patterns, slurs, insults, compliments, curses, magical idioms and dialogue examples. **The original linguistic proposal is canon in full wherever not superseded by later user revisions.** For ordinary conversation, magic is called its **color** (e.g. “shape the white,” “out of black”); the word **mana** remains normal in technical and Shimmer contexts. **Muzzle-mouth** describes an enslaved person frequently muzzled or gagged, and is a strongly derogatory social insult, not a legal class. The Kobold cry **“Oops!”** warns of immediate catastrophe; **“Broken mirrors”** expresses bad luck.

**Kobold racial index resolved November 4:** `02_Races.md` now records Kobolds and their expressly approved base **STR 2 / DEX 8 / END 3 / INT 10 / SPD 7**, with **STR −10%, DEX +10%, INT +20%** racial modifiers. Effective initial total **32.6**. This approval establishes no additional Orbcraft ranks, mana affinity, or starting SP grants.

## Fast mechanics at a glance

| System | Canon pricing / progression |
|---|---|
| Basic attributes | **STR / DEX / END / INT / SPD**; ordinary latest level gain **+2 base stat points**; **+1 every base stat at levels divisible by 5** |
| Tier gates | I / level 1; II / 10; III / 25; IV / 50; V / 80 |
| Ordinary Sorcery | **3 SP per Incantation**, **3 per alteration**; standard one-color 5 + 25 catalogue = **90 SP** |
| Drakyn Sorcery | **2 SP per Incantation**, **2 per alteration**; standard one-color **60 SP** |
| Red Sorcery — resolved November 4 | **25 alterations**: Ignis 6, Varekh 5, Darvok 4, Ashal 5, Ruveth 5; **90 SP ordinary / 60 Drakyn**. Unidentified seventh Ignis purchase removed; all named alterations retained |
| Warlock/Manacraft | **5 skills × 5 ranks × 5 SP = 125 SP**; free Manabending |
| Jestercraft | **6 free foundations + 18 modifiers × 3 + 19 applications × 4 = 130 SP** (126 SP with one Hemweaving ultimate) |
| Native racial Craft | Normally **5 SP per rank**; a five-rank discipline = **25 SP** |
| Naga Psycraft | **3 disciplines × 5 ranks × 5 SP = 75 SP**; free Sovereign Sense |
| Foreign Craft | Awakening **5 SP once**, rank I **7 SP**, later ranks II–V **6 SP each** |
| Drakyn Wyrmcraft | 5 disciplines, 25 ranks = **125 SP native**; all foreign **160 SP** with one awakening |
| Original Centaur Anvilcant | 6 disciplines, 30 ranks = **150 SP**; two branches under redesign |
| Proposed Centaur overhaul | 8 disciplines, 40 ranks = **200 SP** — **NOT CANON YET** |
| Equipment | Five standard levels **14/30/50/70/90 Gems**, ordinary enchanted Soul Strain **1/2/4/6/8** |
| Centaur equipment affinity | **10% off combined equipment Soul Strain** (ceil(0.90 × equipped subtotal) once); **100% ordinary equipment-derived numerical stat bonuses** (no Centaur penalty); other properties unchanged |
| Mana-free races | **Moontouched** natural 0 MP; **Drakyn** natural 0 MP and 0 rest recovery (feed to acquire mana) |

## Primary rules that avoid the largest continuity mistakes

- **Drakyn**, not prior placeholder dragon racial names. Dragon elements arise **in the egg**. Their harvesting changes their current mana color and ejects an older pool into ambient mana.
- **Jestercraft is NOT inversion/Recompense.** A sentient speaking Regalia grants six free foundations, physical Stitches and distinct Cut/Frayed states; Pincushion reverses currently Frayed once/day. Both Hemweaving ultimates may be purchased. Hollow Jesters survive as a lore possibility.
- **Warlocks are not ordinary Sorcerers.** Chosen Moontouched manipulate raw external mana and already-shaped spells; their natural mana pool stays at zero. **Current five skill trees are the November 4 replacements**, not the prior November 3 trees; see `04_Warlock_Manacraft.md`.
- **The Centaur race and Celestite are canon, but the eight-discipline clockwork/Socketwright expansion is still a proposal.** Do not silently bill all Centaur masters 200 SP.
- **Black Sorcery alterations were explicitly approved. Red detailed revisions were explicitly approved.** The former Red 26-purchase ledger is **superseded by the approved 25-alteration resolution (90/60 SP)**. Inaccessible Green/White/Blue final selected names remain open documentation problems, not permission to invent changes.
- **Equipment material, enchantment, Gems, MP and Soul Strain are different mechanisms.** Celestite is **outside normal equipment quality** and destroys a wielder's experience/soul per stroke.
- **Old compendium Shimmer growth of 1 attribute point per level is superseded.** Use 2 base points per level plus +1 all five every fifth level.

- `Mystaria_Warlock_Skill_Costs.md` (earlier cost ledger; use only when not contradicted by full canon reference).

### For other conversations in the project

Open **this index** plus the **dedicated dossier for the subject** before responding. For mechanical disputes, check the **current status** and `10_Canon_Changelog.md` for superseded material. If a named power is missing, request or retrieve the final approved source transcript rather than improvising a "canon" ability.

### How to install these as reusable project references

The files generated in the conversation are **not automatically installed as Project Files**. Download the library ZIP, unpack it, and **add these Markdown files to the Mystaria Lore project's Files section**, replacing old conflicting versions or retaining the earlier compendium only as clearly labeled legacy background. Keeping the full file names and local Markdown links intact makes them easier to identify and cross-reference.

**No automatic synchronization:** Later canon decisions must be deliberately incorporated into the owning Markdown dossier(s), index and changelog.

### Approved visual reference (November 4, 2026)

The five mana symbols are **approved visual canon**: Red = curling fire; Blue = water droplet with inner wave; White = radiant eight-point star; Green = veined leaf; Black = skull with purple forehead jewel. The **actual source artwork**, `Mystaria_Mana_Emblems_Approved.png`, controls design details. See [`11_Visual_Style_Guide.md`](11_Visual_Style_Guide.md) for visual rules and site placement. Artwork captions are not independent mechanical or philosophical canon.

**Latest approved color identity:** Purple is Mystaria's signature publication color and the Shimmer's identifying color. The world's skies tend toward purple, occasionally red. For presentation see `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md`; for in-world sky/Shimmer canon see `01_World_and_Shimmer.md`. No specific palette code or cause for the sky colors has been approved.

### Approved Drider and Centaur illustration style — November 4, 2026

Driders and Centaurs use more anime-inspired character design with **controlled linework, simplified facial features, and expressive posing**. See `11_Visual_Style_Guide.md`, with cross-references in `02_Races.md` and `07_Centaurs_and_Smithcraft.md`. Follow current approved racial anatomy.

## Approved Nymbrin, Wetcraft and Hexen — November 4, 2026
[`20_Nymbrin_Wetcraft_and_Hexen_Canon.md`](20_Nymbrin_Wetcraft_and_Hexen_Canon.md) owns the complete approved Nymbrin race, six Wetcraft domains, Golden Lease, El Dorado/Great Taking, Duskpaw alliance, Wakebound, Pazara/chitin, drugs, passion potions, Hexen and Haze. All current conversation lore is approved, with **Hexen** superseding provisional mutant names. Its later additions control overlapping older summaries.
[`21_Nymbrin_Skill_Options_DRAFT.md`](21_Nymbrin_Skill_Options_DRAFT.md) is a **PROPOSAL**: twelve selectable technique concepts per discipline. No new technique ranks/costs are approved by the lore acceptance. Consumables use Gem-based power and the author's 30% budget design allocation. Centaur's modular overhaul remains noncanon.



## November 5, 2026 — Combat, Endurance, and Shimmer Guard

The approved injury system, combat +10 quadratic comparison, impact thresholds, weapon baselines, Hero and Legend Shimmer Guard, heal burn, firearm principles and terminology are consolidated in [`22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md). **Adamantite** remains the metal's correct spelling; **Crownsalt** is also colloquially called **gunpowder**. **Armor numerical scaling with Centaur crafting remains PROPOSED / UNRESOLVED**, as do precise firearm handling ratios, recoil triggers and short-rest heal-burn recovery. Supersede the previous dagger 0.85× with **dagger 1.0×, gauntlets 1.0× and unarmed 0.85×**. The new dossier owns combat mechanics; existing five-tier equipment prices, Craft ranks and costs are unchanged.

## November 5, 2026 — Combat terminology

The approved combat vocabulary is **Power** (general scaling result, not necessarily damage), **Resistance** (effective END before the +10 combat offset), and **Defense** (withstanding an effect, not evasion). Specific resistances organize threat-specific modifiers; evasion is separate. The owning reference is [`22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md). Existing quadratic formulas and impact thresholds are unchanged; exact mixed-modifier resolution, armor scaling, casting-implement bonuses and vulnerability stacking remain unresolved.


## November 5, 2026 — Speed, pressure and evasion

The approved Speed design in [`22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`](22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md) uses **six hit-difficulty categories (Certain / Easy / Average / Hard / Very Hard / Impossible)** based on `(defender effective SPD + 10)/(attacker effective SPD + 10)`, adjudicated in four pressure situations (**Open / Pressured / Trapped / Extreme Pressure**). Evasion is separate from Resistance, does not by itself add attacks, and can fully negate a hit; rarer glances mitigate roughly half the **injury states**. Armor penalizes SPD (rates unresolved), with area denial and coordinated attackers as counters. Extreme mismatches permit **Shimmer Guard projected evasion paths** as a conditional DM tool; exact numerical benefit is unresolved. Previous percentage dodge model **not for normal gameplay**, only rare hidden balance testing. **Raising every racial starting stat by 10 is merely a PROPOSAL**, not canon; retain the existing combat-only +10 and all original magic range/MP formulas.

## November 5, 2026 — Approved racial stat +10 rebase (latest)

**NEW CONTROLLING CANON:** All approved Hero racial **base** STR/DEX/END/INT/SPD increase by **+10 before racial multipliers**, with a universal absolute **minimum attribute of 1**; non-Hero monsters may have attributes below 10. Original stat gains per level remain **+2 allocatable base points** and **+1 all base stats every fifth level**. Effective milestones become **35 and 110**. The earlier **combat-only +10 offset is superseded**: use squared **new effective** STR/Resistance and direct new effective Speed ratio for the same injury and evasion bands. Natural MP intentionally rises for generating races (Moontouched/Drakyn remain 0); Channel Limits and stat-based magical reach generally use **`max(1, new effective INT − 10)`** to preserve early reach (e.g. new INT 11 gives 3 m ordinary Tidekin Hydrokinesis and 4 m Warlock Manabending). Warlock temporary one-hand MP uses similarly adjusted END, not a free handling increase. **All existing mana-based costs need later rebalancing review; no costs changed as part of this migration**. Approved quadratic lift benchmark `100 × (STR/15)^2` lb one-time waist-height. Revised source owners: `01_World_and_Shimmer.md`, `02_Races.md`, `04_Warlock_Manacraft.md`, `07_Centaurs_and_Smithcraft.md`, `13_Wakecraft_The_Six_Arts_of_the_Living_Tide.md`, `14_Vren_Sanguine_Arts_Canon.md`, `15_Naga_Psycraft_Canon.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`. Historic lower tables/examples elsewhere are superseded.


## November 5, 2026 — Dexterity, Proficiencies and movement amendments (LATEST)

`22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md` owns the approved distinction **DEX = physical anticipation, precision, stealth, counter-stealth** (never open-ground invisibility) and **SPD = reaction/movement**. Successful player-driven predictions apply at **DEX ≥ 75% of target SPD**, with **no wrong-prediction-specific penalty at ≥ 200%** due to built-in contingencies. Non-Hero mortals improve stats through training, **Heroes only through Shimmer level/experience growth**. Marathon-capable END reference revised **15 → 25**. Extraordinary movement-assisted attacks may gain modest Power with actual relative closing velocity and sufficient run-up; matching opponent velocity nullifies the boost. **Dash**, **sprint**, **jog** are respectively peak, one and two chart tiers below. **Exact speed tiers and illustrative +25% Power near 100 mph / +50% near 500 mph remain proposals**, not finalized numeric combat rules. **PROFICIENCIES** is a **NONCANON, undeveloped** proposed system of attribute-gated SP-purchasable mundane expertise such as lockpicking. Revisit for imported/general RPG skill-system design, **not** Mystaria's magical Skills/Crafts.

## November 5, 2026 — Modular Anvilcant and devotional reservoirs (latest)

The author approved eight Anvilcant disciplines (Orewhisper, Oathsteel, Thunderforge, Hearthsong, Socketwright, Clockwright, Cogmarshal, Hostforge), 40 ranks at 5 SP each, and retired Formwright/Monumentcraft as purchasable trees. New active rank mechanics remain subject to review. Oathsteel natural INT/END crafting minima are 18/20, 26/30, 40/45, 60/65, 90/90 by quality. Cogmind Calamity and ordinary clockwork's lack of independent thought are approved lore. Fully modular equipment is the governing design; independent Moonlit Forge +1–+5 reinforcement is retired. Divine mana reservoirs recharge through compatible-affinity sincere prayer toward anyone, not only goddesses; approximately 1 MP per 10 END per turn at full devotion and one-tenth that for unenthusiastic prayer are design targets, with rounding and capacity still open. Weapon STR/DEX/either/both directions, quality-reduced requirements, coverage, burden and Soul Strain budgets are approved philosophies but exact numerical implementation, runes, names and item slots are unresolved. See `07_Centaurs_and_Smithcraft.md`, `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, and the latest Changelog.


## November 5, 2026 — Passive elemental runes: chosen damage method

The author approved **Conditional Contact Effect (model B)** for passive elemental weapon runes. An appropriately connected weapon attack, including a nonpenetrating armored hit, may impart a weaker resisted elemental contact consequence; complete evasion gives none. Preserve elemental material interactions, superior type-specific resistance, rank-V elemental transformation **replacing the original material property**, and separate active Sorcery gem activation. **Specific rank numbers, resistance modifiers, rider injury limits/durations and exact hex doses remain UNAPPROVED PROPOSALS** in `24_Modular_Equipment_Design_Options_DRAFT.md`. Subject references: `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, and Nymbrin hex canon `20_Nymbrin_Wetcraft_and_Hexen_Canon.md`.


## November 5, 2026 — Elemental Magnitude, faithwells and clockwork follow-up (LATEST)

**Approved:** Leywell is also called a **faithwell** by religious people (alongside common *well*). Portable **Command Loom prices 12/28/55/90/140 Gems** by Cogmarshal rank I–V, with capacity/range/upkeep unresolved. **Construct chassis materials and engineering are the source of construct basic stats**, unlike a magic sword; integrate the existing **Windling, Quickstep, Iron Servitor, Bulwark, Gearsteed, Siege Engine** design candidates with Leywells and rune/gem systems **after** other crafting is finished. **Elemental Magnitude** replaces *Contact Pressure* as the broader energy-attack/magic intensity and resistance notation, particularly for Sorcery, respecting Sorcery-specific INT/MP/spell scaling; actual spell values and statuses unapproved. Elemental Status will have **three ranks**, separate from item-rune I–V, and eligible rank-III passive effects may inflict resisted conditions that lower their own-element Resistance. Wet targets favor lightning contact where conduction exists. **Immunity to a physical/magical energy type generally means near-immunity**, not absolute invincibility, including fire Drakyn's heat resistance. Rank-V all-element weapons retain their appropriate normal weapon Power plus limited passive rider, **replacing original material special property**. Specific status effects/formulas remain proposals. Owners: `01_World_and_Shimmer.md`, `03_Sorcery.md`, `06_Drakyn.md`, `07_Centaurs_and_Smithcraft.md`, `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`; draft `24_Modular_Equipment_Design_Options_DRAFT.md`.

## November 5, 2026 — World name and squad commands

Mystaria is the canonical world name; any earlier Cindara reference to the whole world was erroneous. Lightning Shocked I/II/III and Water/Air displacement goals have new author-stated targets. Hex is being developed as anti-caster and anti-magical technology, with numeric rules still unresolved. Several clockwork allies should provide tactical options through coordinated commander-issued actions rather than independent full combat turns. Detailed designs remain proposals. See `07_Centaurs_and_Smithcraft.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, and the Changelog.

## November 6, 2026 — Cogsong and Cogmarshal squad rules (LATEST)

**Cogsong** is the approved name for clockwork's music-box-like mechanically encoded acoustic Starsong, distinct from Kobold Cipherlight. A **Command Loom is mandatory** for Cogmarshal control; owning one does not grant Cogmarshal ranks. Existing Loom prices **12/28/55/90/140 Gems** remain canon; optional-Loom/native-CP doubling proposals are superseded. Standard homogeneous squad size **4 constructs**. Reinforcing one ordinary squad to **5–8 constructs costs +1 CP** and still yields only one combined attack per command; above eight requires separate squads. **Windlings are the exception: 1 CP commands 4 Windlings**, scaling to impressive swarms without ordinary squad reinforcement surcharges or per-Windling full attack turns. Simple standing instructions persist until completed; every fresh attack requires a new meaningful commander order/action. Cogmarshal CP-by-rank capacities, non-Windling squad base costs, acoustic range, MP upkeep, swarm pressure and material-derived construct stat blocks remain unresolved. Owners: `07_Centaurs_and_Smithcraft.md` (command and crafting), `12_Languages_and_Linguistic_Customs.md` (Cogsong), `10_Canon_Changelog.md` (precedence), `24_Modular_Equipment_Design_Options_DRAFT.md` (proposals).

## November 6, 2026 — Approved Cogmarshal CP capacity (LATEST)

**Cogmarshal I–V has 2/4/6/8/12 Command Points with a mandatory functioning compatible Cogsong Command Loom.** This formerly proposed table is now **APPROVED**, not optional-Loom doubling or a no-Loom native capacity. CP measures controlled formation capacity, not extra combat actions. At the established rate of **4 Windlings per CP**, dedicating all CP to Windlings allows **8/16/24/32/48 Windlings** at ranks I–V, respectively, without granting individual Windlings separate attacks. Ordinary four-unit homogeneous squads and +1 CP reinforcement to five–eight remain canon. Exact non-Windling CP prices and Windling pressure/screening/attrition mechanics remain proposals. See `07_Centaurs_and_Smithcraft.md`, `10_Canon_Changelog.md` and `24_Modular_Equipment_Design_Options_DRAFT.md`.

## November 6, 2026 — Windling pressure, casualties and sonic weapon (LATEST)

**APPROVED:** mandatory-Loom Cogmarshal CP I–V **2/4/6/8/12**; 4 Windlings per CP. Windlings specialize in **flexible tactical saturation** (harass, screen, distract, lift). All squads gain additional tactical abilities from increased numbers, not independent extra attacks. Pressure must be **earned through stat comparison** and a bounded coordination multiplier; a weak swarm cannot automatically pressure a legendary fast or unthreatened target. Ordinary small/medium constructs lose a **number of units per covered, sufficiently powerful attack**, rather than each tracking independent full injury states. Wide greatsword sweeps and narrated rapid dagger cuts may disable multiple units **within one existing attack action**; narrow rifle shots usually affect one. The largest Siege Engines instead keep individual injury states. Windlings have a **sonic music box** weapon designed not to interfere with Cogsong frequencies; painful, typically nonlethal hearing damage to living targets, and slower sustained sonic receiver damage against constructs that can sever incoming orders. All numerical stat multipliers, cutoff thresholds, sonic status/duration, material-derived construct stats, coverage and squad unlocks are **UNAPPROVED**. See `07_Centaurs_and_Smithcraft.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, `12_Languages_and_Linguistic_Customs.md`, `24_Modular_Equipment_Design_Options_DRAFT.md`.

## November 6, 2026 — Windling targeted sonic attack, up to three turns (LATEST)

**Approved:** One Windling swarm **Cogsong attack order** commits the group to **targeted sonic distraction of one specified creature for up to THREE turns**. The bounded sustained pursuit is one offensive command, not three independent new attacks, and expires even if interrupted or unable to reach its target. Attacking a new target or beginning another pursuit requires another commander action. Standard Windling music-box attacks are **extremely short-range**; standing approximately **five meters away from the swarm generally avoids fresh sonic exposure**. Melee counterattacks, escaping, physical acoustic barriers and protecting/covering ears are valid defenses. Effective pressure still requires stat-relative capability, bounded size multiplier and vulnerability. No automatic damage while screening or carrying. Command frequencies remain designed to avoid collision with the weapon's harmful acoustic output; receiver disruption requires sustained exposure and does not inherently destroy construct chassis. Exact hearing severity, actual attack stat comparisons, signal/resistance numbers and initiative details remain UNAPPROVED. See `07_Centaurs_and_Smithcraft.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, `12_Languages_and_Linguistic_Customs.md`.

## November 6, 2026 — Weapon shapes and generic horde combat (LATEST)

Equipment crafting review begins with **Light/Medium/Heavy** melee weapon forms, **primary plus explicitly supported alternate** Piercing/Slashing/Blunt actions, and separate Reach versus Coverage. **User-approved qualitative ratings:** Piercing Injury **1/5** and Armor Bypass **5/5**, Slashing Injury **5/5** and Armor Bypass **1/5** (maximum coverage), Blunt Injury **3/5** and Armor Bypass **3/5** (middle coverage). Blunt uses reduced effective injury severity **without a one-state hard cap**. Secondary-weapon **Attack** allocation makes an opponent harder to evade; secondary-weapon **Defense** allocation aids the wielder's evasion, with actual numerical modifier pending. Sufficient STR permits one-handed normally-heavy and dual-heavy weapons; **150%/200% proposed handling benchmarks** are selected for working calibration, not fully specified universal material/geometry rules. Shields occupy a hand and increase appropriate Defense; nuanced armor pass to follow.

**GENERAL COMBAT DESIGN:** Multi-target slashing/coverage, physical Power and group casualties must extend to monster **hordes, gangs, armies** as well as clockwork. Revisit general horde enemies as statistically organized combat groups/squads, maintaining stat-gated pressure and exceptional Hero/Legend handling. Exact weapon list, form multipliers, armor bypass percentages, numeric reach, coverage count, shield block and dual-wield effects are NOT YET CANON. Owners: `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`; draft `24_Modular_Equipment_Design_Options_DRAFT.md`.

## November 6, 2026 — Short, Medium and Long reach (LATEST)

**APPROVED:** Weapon handling Light/Medium/Heavy is separate from weapon Reach **Short/Medium/Long** and attack Coverage. Short reach works in tight spaces and grapples; Medium balances; properly controlled Long reach can deny ordinary Short-reach attacks until a close-in succeeds, while making Medium-reach approaches harder. Meaningful Pressure against the long-weapon wielder removes its controlling reach benefit. A Short fighter who grapples inside the Long weapon's functional distance cannot ordinarily be struck with that weapon until the long wielder escapes, regains spacing or has a specifically applicable technique. **Choking Up** as future mundane Proficiency remains only a proposal. **Shields** occupy a hand and have three approved intended roles: passive local protection, active directional block/parry and contextual evasive maneuver, with numeric armor details still open. **Light weapon = 1.00× baseline attack-rating reference** supersedes the old fixed dagger 1.00× placeholder; individual dagger values remain to be developed. Exact armor bypass percentages deferred until armor. **Proposed, not approved, Coverage vocabulary**: Point, Narrow, Sweep, Wide Sweep, Line. Horde/army general combat still to be developed. Owning files `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.


## November 6, 2026 — Approved coverage and melee attribute Power model (LATEST)

The five approved attack Coverage categories are **Point, Narrow, Sweep, Wide Sweep, Line** (attack geometry), separate from Reach **Short/Medium/Long** and handling class **Light/Medium/Heavy**. Weapon Power for ordinary applicable melee attacks follows **`(A × F)^2`**, with `A = STR / DEX / max(STR,DEX) / 0.65(STR+DEX)` according to a weapon's declared STR, DEX, Either or Both attribute profile; the Both option requires satisfying **both minimum stats**. `F` is the correctly calibrated weapon attack-rating modifier, without double-counting. Use post-rebase effective stats, no historical additive combat +10. The baseline Light attack-rating reference is 1.00, not an immutable dagger-specific 1.00. Exact individual F and requirements still need approval. Entry past a Long weapon uses **opportunity and execution, not feet/meters**, and a reckless willingness to be stabbed never guarantees access; sufficient defender SPD and accurate existing-system DEX prediction help maintain reach control. **Defender SPD >=1.5× attacker SPD is merely a suggested, not approved, reach-control cutoff**. Approved Short-grapple and Pressure exception rules remain. Keep the open/inside/grapple dagger-vs-spear illustration for future explanation. Owners: `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, `10_Canon_Changelog.md`; numerical proposals `24_Modular_Equipment_Design_Options_DRAFT.md`.

## November 6, 2026 — Reach Model C approved; ranged shapes next (LATEST)

**APPROVED:** A ready Long-reach defender's effective SPD advantage against a Short attacker seeking close combat follows **Model C**: **<1.25×** no special speed threshold, **1.25× to <1.5×** direct entry harder, **>=1.5×** can deny straightforward unsupported rush when reach control is maintained. Real Pressure and earned tactical opportunities can defeat this control; DEX anticipation uses already-approved player-prediction rules, not a passive bonus. No feet/meters measure for entry, and no extra attacks. Prior numerical-proposal labels for these thresholds superseded. Melee shape architecture and coverage names are approved, while exact individual weapon multipliers, handling minima, attack coverage counts and armor bypass remain unresolved. Ranged shape design next: bows, crossbows, firearms, thrown/manual weapons, and mounted artillery are under proposal review, not approved ranged taxonomy. Owners `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`; draft `24_Modular_Equipment_Design_Options_DRAFT.md`.

## November 6, 2026 — Approved ranged families, battle saddles and capped-draw bows (LATEST)

**Ranged family classification:** Bows, Crossbows, Firearms, Thrown/Manual, Mounted/Siege. **Range bands:** Close/Effective/Far/Extreme relative to each weapon. **Bow Model A** approved: shooter STR contributes actual draw capacity **capped by construction**, DEX governs aiming. Crossbows use stored mechanism energy; firearms and artillery intrinsic gun/propellant/projectile Power, **not direct shooter STR damage**; thrown weapons use suitable STR/DEX physical forms. Recoil/weight matter to STR; DEX builds favor lightweight weapons and precision. **Battle saddles:** body-mounted gun or mechanically operated crossbow configurations for Centaurs, Crownform Drakyn and applicable Firstshaped, with no inherent extra attacks or projectile Power; Centaurs may pull cannon carts, but towing is not recoil stabilization. Drakyn Crownform has manipulative forelegs, not upright shooting stance. **Adamantite** is heavy/reinforcing, **Trufula** lightweight yet extremely flammable and not automatically safe for Crownsalt firearms. Numerics, range distances, ammo, saddle recipes, reload and recoil support unresolved. Owners `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, `06_Drakyn.md`, `07_Centaurs_and_Smithcraft.md`; draft `24_Modular_Equipment_Design_Options_DRAFT.md`.

## November 6, 2026 — Three-point Pressure and ranged reload/aim (LATEST)

**APPROVED:** meaningful **net Pressure Points**: **0 Open, 1–2 Pressured (no difference), 3+ Extreme Pressure (no stacking beyond threshold)**; **Trapped is an independent physical/positional condition, NOT 3 PP**, disabling ordinary dodge against the relevant attack while aware target retains legal defensive responses. Only stat-qualified meaningful sources count; typical credible flank, DEX prediction, small archery squad suppression, reach and offhand attack generate ~1 PP; defensive counterplay may remove qualifying PP. Windling group of four may provide 1 if actually capable, but a lone swarm requires **nonlinearly/exponentially greater effective scale** to reach 3 PP than to reach 1. Exact stat multiplier/threshold unfinished, and no free PP per 4 Windlings.

**RELOAD:** ordinary bow nock/draw gives no distinctive loading opening; light crossbow modest loading vulnerability; careful single-shot pistol or high-capacity-magazine loading costs full-turn action; siege loading leaves major openings, detailed engine cycles TBD. **AIM:** one meaningful ranged action aiming in place of attacking improves later hit reliability, not shot damage; normal two-action comparison yields one aimed shot instead of two ordinary attacks. **MOUNTS:** fixed, articulated, heavy supported and assisted-loading configurations approved, no extra attacks/recoil erasure. **Proficiencies future:** reload-time and reload-vulnerability techniques only proposed; not purchased skills. Owners `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `10_Canon_Changelog.md`; draft `24_Modular_Equipment_Design_Options_DRAFT.md`.

## November 6, 2026 — Rapid-fire and pressure modifiers (LATEST)

**Approved:** Net meaningful 0 PP = baseline evasion, 1–2 PP = one hit-difficulty category easier to land, 3+ PP = two categories easier, capped. Trapped remains independent and an aware target still has legal other defenses. Aim trades an attack action for one category improvement on a later valid shot, not another independent PP. Capable rapid-fire equipment spends additional ammunition for qualified ranged suppression or substantial close-range Power increase as two alternative modes within the standard attack action, not per-projectile extra attack actions. Exact PP, burst damage, rounds and recoil are not final. Draft tests: +1 PP at range or +35% final physical Power close, not approved. Owning files `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`.


## November 6, 2026 — Full-auto numerical canon and ranged weapon ammo data (LATEST)

**Approved:** **Suppressive Fire +1 stat-qualified PP** at range by expending a listed full-auto volley; **Concentrated Burst ×1.35 FINAL physical attack Power** at close range with viable hits and ammunition expenditure, not attack rating ×1.35. These two options and same user's offhand Attack/Defense weapon tactics **do not stack**: two automatic pistols do not double pressure or burst damage, and no full attack per bullet. Each gun must list loaded **ammunition capacity**; automatic firearms must additionally list **full-auto volley cost**. Standard magazines cannot fire multiple full volleys without reloading; upgraded magazines/feed systems may. Exact cartridge counts and upgrade engineering still open. Future general mundane Proficiencies: candidate capability to apply rapid-fire-like techniques to ordinarily nonautomatic weapons such as bows, alongside reload time/protection proposals; no SP costs or rank mechanics canon. Next detailed ranged phase is handling and recoil, then individual ranged profiles/materials. Owning refs `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`; changelog `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.

## November 6, 2026 — Point Blank burst and attachment handling (LATEST)

**New controlling rule:** Concentrated Burst receives its approved **+35% FINAL physical Power** at **Point Blank, normally up to 10 meters**, only when the full volley can substantially connect despite recoil and other genuine conditions. A **rare GM exception** may extend the distance for a truly massive target whose geometry allows effectively all projectiles to connect; this is not a general balancing benchmark. Point Blank is separate from subjective weapon-relative Close/Effective/Far/Extreme bands. Larger magazines and certain modifications may raise STR and/or DEX requirements; sophisticated scopes may improve usable range/precision but require corresponding DEX skill to exploit fully. Exact attachment thresholds and recoil/support handling remain open. Owners `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`; details in `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.

## November 6, 2026 — Firearm handling triad (LATEST)

**Approved:** Every gun profile must distinguish Support STR, Recoil STR and Handling DEX from intrinsic shot Damage Rating, and classify unsupported, braced, fitted battle-saddle/body-mounted, and deployed carriage support. Recoil and mass are physically redistributed by supports, not ignored; a cart is not a deployed cannon brace. Moderate deficits may impair operation without preventing all trigger pulls, and extreme genuinely injurious recoil already advances one injury state. Exact requirement ratios and penalties remain **PROPOSED** (candidate >=100% normal, 75–<100% strained, 50–<75% severe, <50% unsafe unaided). Numeric mount effects, per-gun profiles and material/quality work follow. Owning refs `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`.

## November 6, 2026 — Recoil Strain and ranged Cover (LATEST)

**Approved:** Recoil Strain persists across consecutive shots and resets after one complete turn **without firing**, even when running or casting. 0/1/2/3+ Strain produces 0/1/2/3 PP **against the shooter**; **no DEX/precision reduction**. Full recoil/weapon requirement stat deficits and maximum one recoil injury state per discharge/volley remain separately approved in PR #48. Exact strain gained per shot and support control remain proposals.

**Cover, separate from PP:** Normal cover, for example waist-high rock, makes a viable incoming ranged attack **1 category harder**; Full cover, for example peeking around brick wall, **2 harder**; Amazing cover, usually arrow slit/tiny aperture, **3 harder**. If a wall blocks every line of fire to all exposed target areas, no ordinary direct shot is possible. Pressure and cover are independent shifts of the existing six-category hit ladder, not extra pressure sources. Owns `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`; equipment cross-reference `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`; status `10_Canon_Changelog.md`; calibration draft `24_Modular_Equipment_Design_Options_DRAFT.md`.


## November 6, 2026 — Recoil generation approved; weapon shapes to finalize (LATEST)

**Approved shot/volley Recoil Strain additions by wielder effective STR ÷ supported Recoil STR requirement:** >=125% **0/1**; 100–<125% **1/2**; 75–<100% **1/2**; 50–<75% **2/3**; below 50% **unsafe unsupported**. Ordinary shot listed first, full-auto volley second. This table is NOW CANON, replacing former proposal labels in `24_Modular_Equipment_Design_Options_DRAFT.md`. Existing recoil 0/1/2/3+ PP against shooter, one nonshooting-turn reset, no DEX debuff, separate STR deficit SPD and injury maximum one state per firing event persist.

## November 6, 2026 — APPROVED armor shape, strength, rear vulnerability and acid design checkpoint (reconciled)

**LATEST AUTHOR-APPROVED ARMOR:** Protective construction classes **Light / Medium / Heavy** are distinct from protective **coverage Partial / Standard / Full** (the former draft term "Comprehensive" is superseded by **Full**). All three coverage extents have the class's full main-surface Armor Rating wherever protected, not a coverage-dependent AR. Conventional reference-material ratings: **Light AR 25, Medium AR 45, Heavy AR 75**. Thus broad Full Light armor can cover the entire wearer without becoming Medium. Medium/Heavy designs include **built-in, nonstacking Light-equivalent underarmor (AR 25)** at protected joints/structural gaps. A legitimately struck truly exposed opening, including face or eyes, receives **AR 0**. No separately purchased/tracked under-plate light suit; treat the outfit as one ensemble for future mobility/STR burden, not individual footwear or glove burden. A selectively stripped Partial Heavy suit retains heavy vital protection but can later trade exposed peripherals for mobility.

**APPROVED QUALIFYING REAR BONUS:** A conventional attack only qualifies to hit the exposed rear through **surprise or actually earned rear-exposing positional opportunity/Pressure**, not merely standing behind an alert opponent. A legitimate rear hit adds **+10 percentage points to applicable armor penetration**, capped at 100% (e.g. 50% to 60%), before squaring remaining armor in Defense Power. **The former -15% rear AR candidate is rejected.**

**AUTHOR-APPROVED DM/ECONOMY INSTRUCTION:** Because defensive armor power is quadratic, armor is very strong at low levels, especially full Heavy armor. Normal **price and availability** should delay ordinary acquisition until enemies can meaningfully contest it. This is not a new fixed use-level gate or an approved Gem price; rare early armor treasure can still be possible, with serious encounter-balancing implications.

**FUTURE ENCHANTMENT REQUIREMENT:** **Acid** is an attack element/effect for future enchantment design which must **lower Armor Rating, but never below AR 0**. No corrosion amount/duration/stacking/repair/material interaction or extra mana color is approved.

**STILL UNRESOLVED:** Numeric attack-form/pike penetration (0/50/65% examples were tests only), armor piece targeting, precise armor-carry STR and SPD burden for the nine ensembles, shields, armor material/quality variations, armor prices/availability numbers, acid corrosion mechanics. The earlier proposal-only AR 25/45/75 status is **superseded by this explicit approval**. Detailed owner files: `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`; status: `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.


## November 6, 2026 — Shield architecture approved, durability damage tests pending

**LATEST USER-APPROVED SHIELD SYSTEM DESIGN:** Shields provide **their own independent defensive capacity**, governed by shield shape, material, craftsmanship and later enchantment; do **not** simply add shield protection to the wearer's body Armor Rating or END². Shields track **three accumulated durability losses before breaking** (names: **Intact 0/3 → Dented 1/3 → Cracked 2/3 → Broken 3/3**; the previously suggested state **Strained** is explicitly replaced by **Dented**). No reduction in protection, obstruction, blocking performance or other shield function at 1/3 or 2/3; **Broken shields are useless until repaired**. Exceptionally powerful attacks may cause multiple durability losses in a single impact; weak attacks below the shield's meaningful defensive threshold should not automatically chip away durability. Shield durability damage, breakthrough/overflow and shield-defense numerical formula still require testing and approval.

**APPROVED SHIELD POSITIONING:** A properly handled, intact shield makes it harder to attack an actually shielded fighter's exposed body regions **other than the shield itself**. Recognized shape roles: **buckler** (quick parries/minimal static coverage), **standard shield** (balanced), **tower shield** (broad coverage, more cumbersome); proposed ordinary baseline shield obstruction **+1 / +1 / +2 six-tier hit-difficulty categories** respectively, only for plausible defensive angles while able to operate the shield. This is not universal body AR and is not automatically additive to other benefits representing the same obstruction. Shield handling has its **own STR/DEX requirements**, not yet numerical. Shield can physically intercept attacks and take its own durability damage; avoid simultaneously claiming all passive, active and evasive benefits for the same movement.

**LATEST AUTHOR-APPROVED EXTREME PRESSURE EXCEPTION:** At **net 3+ meaningful PP (Extreme Pressure)** the defender **cannot actively block/parry with a shield or claim the shield's maneuver-based / active obstruction hit-difficulty benefit**. Shield material remains in the world and physically intercepts attacks that actually strike its existing position. This is a shield-specific limitation and **does not generally equate Extreme Pressure with Trapped, or prohibit other defenses**; **Trapped below Extreme Pressure** may still permit physically credible shield responses. Shields need **their own future enchantments**, likely shared with or closely analogous to armor enchantments; no new rune abilities, prices, Soul Strain or slots approved. See `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.


## November 6, 2026 — Shield breakpoint direction, independent requirements, repair/economy backlog and Nymbrin hard materials

**LATEST AUTHOR-APPROVED DIRECTIONS:** When a shield **breaks while intercepting an incoming physical strike**, breakthrough/overflow should carry toward the defender, **ordinarily the torso**, instead of disappearing or automatically striking a different exposed body area. The shield should still absorb meaningful Power before overflow; **the exact formula and overflow injury handling remain for testing**, not yet approved. The user endorses earlier **shape baseline Shield Ratings Buckler 30 / Standard 50 / Tower 75 and the 0/1/2/3 durability-loss impact threshold test (ratio below 1 / 1–<2 / 2–<3 / 3+) as testing candidates**, not finished item/material canon; these do not change 3-state Dented/Cracked/Broken fully functional until Broken rules. Shield **STR requirements should resemble armor support requirements** and **DEX requirements should resemble simple weapon handling**, creating meaningful limits for single-stat casters. Exact per-shape STR/DEX and deficient-use mechanics **still proposals**.

**ECONOMY/REPAIR BACKLOG:** Defer exact mundane shield and weapon repair mechanics, costs, labor and availability to the later mundane economy workstream; do not invent a Gem-price ladder now. Repair remains required for Broken shield usability.

**NYMBRIN WETCRAFT RESISTANCE DIRECTIVE:** When designing Nymbrin Reshaping and related matter-affecting magic, **shields, purpose-made hard armor and hard protective materials must be more difficult to alter than ordinary stone or weak substances**, with material integrity/resistance, craftsmanship and suitable magic relevant. Preserve Wetcraft's existing **equivalent exchange, weight/constituent/complexity constraints, resources, and Hex production**; do not imply unlimited effortless transmutation or invent numeric resistance levels. Subject owner `20_Nymbrin_Wetcraft_and_Hexen_Canon.md`; combat and equipment owners `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`. Shield and armor enchantments should be designed together **at the end of the armor workstream** and may share effects or close analogues. Numerical shield breakthrough/proposed STR/DEX tests remain unapproved.


## November 8, 2026 — APPROVED shield overflow: remaining durability absorption

**LATEST USER-APPROVED OVERFLOW FORMULA:** For a shield that successfully intercepts a physical blow, determine durability loss using the ongoing **test baseline** `Shield Impact = Incoming Power / Shield Rating²` with bands below 1 = 0; 1–<2 = 1; 2–<3 = 2; 3+ = 3. Count remaining durability **before** this strike: `R = 3 − existing losses` (Intact 3, Dented 2, Cracked 1). **Only if this blow causes Broken (3 accumulated losses)**, pass `Overflow Power = max(0, Incoming Power − R × Shield Rating²)` toward the defender's **torso**; otherwise overflow is zero. Resolve torso overflow once against effective END² plus penetrated torso AR² and ordinary injury/Shimmer rules. This **supersedes** the previous proposed subtraction of only `Shield Rating²` regardless of previous durability. Dented/Cracked shields still perform identically to Intact shields until Broken; R measures destruction capacity, not a weakened blocking stat. **The numerical shape Shield Ratings 30/50/75, shield durability-loss thresholds, and STR/DEX minimums remain calibration baselines, not final approved equipment statistics.** User also directed playtesting to monitor whether even this shield arithmetic creates an undesirable calculator burden; avoid turning combat into frequent wound-calculator interruptions. Owners `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md` and `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`; history `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.


## November 8, 2026 — Equipment Load Synergy and visible shortfall penalties APPROVED

**APPROVED WORKING LOADOUT SYNERGY:** Shield handling **STR requirement** reduction when wearing no armor **−25%**, Light armor **−20%**, Medium armor **−10%**, Heavy armor **0%**; Heavy armor handling STR requirement with genuinely free hands and no significant held weapon/shield **−20%**. Round effective required STR up to an integer. These act on **requirements** rather than stats; never stack overlapping discount claims or exploit momentary dropping of equipment. Inherent armor mobility burden is separately resolved in its future numeric stage. The percentages are approved as **current equipment calibration rules**, subject to playtest; item-specific baseline minima remain draft.

**APPROVED EQUIPMENT SHORTFALL:** Compare current effective STR and DEX (after legitimate racial/item/injury effects) against relevant post-synergy requirements. A shield with both ratios **>=75%** functions fully; below 75% of either it cannot ordinarily be wielded in handheld combat. For shortfall across applicable weapon, armor and shield handling stats, use **only the greatest fractional deficit** and impose **2% situational SPD reduction per 1% shortfall**. Do not stack competing requirements' shortfall penalties, penalize both Support and Recoil twice, or double-count existing Recoil Strain PP. Injury can trigger a new shortfall; final effective SPD cannot drop below 1. Keep class-specific equipment unsafe-use constraints and deliberate shield-block impossibility under Extreme Pressure.

**MANDATORY FUTURE CHARACTER SHEET:** A prominently **RED lettered equipment-shortfall warning** must explicitly list affected item(s), deficient STR/DEX requirements, current effective stat, shortage percentage and total applied SPD penalty, mark the **highest driving deficit** and explain wound-induced changes, rather than silently subtract SPD. The warnings must update on loadout, injury, stat buffs and support changes. Subject owners `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`; update `10_Canon_Changelog.md`. Numerical shield ratings, shield STR/DEX profile, armor-burden matrix and other item-specific numeric floors remain in testing.

## November 8, 2026 — Speed initiative and synchronized teamwork (approved)

**LATEST AUTHOR-APPROVED COMBAT PROCEDURE:** Initiative order uses **current effective SPD, highest first**. Tied **Heroes/Legends act before ordinary combatants**; **player characters win an equal-SPD tie with NPC Heroes/Legends**. Other identical-priority ties are still procedural open questions; do not invent a roll. Armor, wounds and gear-shortfall SPD modifiers apply normally to initiative. A faster actor can **delay its action to a slower teammate's initiative** to coordinate a valid simultaneous threat, with real opportunity cost and no extra attacks or automatic Pressure. A shield user cannot actively intercept **both a simultaneous frontal and rear attack** with the same shield during one exchange, even at 0–2 PP; only one opposed facing can receive the block. The other attack is not automatically a hit or entitled to an unearned exposed-rear bonus. Under 3+ PP, deliberate shield blocking remains unavailable while already-placed physical shield collision is still possible. Apply realistic geometry and independent stat-credible coordination to melee, ranged, grapples or spells. Exact delay/interruption action economy remains unresolved. Detailed owners `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md` and `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`; approval `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.


## November 8, 2026 — Initiative exact ties and final reference armor-form pass approved

**LATEST USER APPROVAL:** For still-tied combatants after current effective SPD and player/Hero/Legend priorities, **the DM makes the final initiative ordering decision** (coin flip or rock-paper-scissors encouraged for fun), which remains **fixed thereafter** rather than re-determined each round. Voluntary tactical delaying does not rewrite that established order.

**Historical 65% entry:** The separate specialized profile has been retired. **APPROVED REFERENCE-MATERIAL ATTACK FORM ARMOR BYPASS:** **Slashing 0%, Blunt 25%, ordinary Piercing 50%, specialized genuinely armor-piercing point 65%**. Apply to actual covered AR before squaring; earned rear hit adds 10 penetration percentage points, not an extra free hit. The special class depends on appropriate purpose-built attack geometry, not the word Piercing or Line. **Approved working weak-spot categories:** actual exposed body region +1 category to hit / AR0 if precision necessary; protected joint/gap +2 / built-in AR25; exceptionally tiny actual uncovered opening +3 / AR0. Impossible geometry cannot be targeted. Preserve ordinary Light25/Medium45/Heavy75 surface AR, and don't double-count a weak joint's lower AR and bonus bypass or cover/evasion for a single cause. These values are **conventional calibration rules to retest after materials/enchantments** and don't finalize form-specific injury modifiers.

**APPROVED SHIELD BLOCK CLARIFICATION:** At net 0–2 meaningful PP, valid aware qualified shieldbearing can actively intercept a physically reachable threat regardless of SPD hit-difficulty tier, but not in every direction at once and without a separate automatic second roll or passive/active stacking for one motion. At net 3+ PP deliberate blocking disabled, while literal shield collision remains possible; simultaneous front/rear threats admit only one actively shielded facing. Existing shield durability and torso-overflow canon unchanged. Final exceptional shield reaction timing is deferred to action-economy review. Subjects `22_Combat_Endurance_and_Shimmer_Guard.md`, `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`; provenance `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.

Under approved Smithcraft quality architecture, workmanship already helps reduce physical stat requirements, though the actual tier discounts are unsettled. The author is now evaluating Quill's proposed **additional direct equipment-performance benefits** from quality: controlled weapon single-F increases, improved Armor Rating from physical construction and independently improved shield Rating, with carefully bounded interactions with existing I–V equipment stat bonuses, runes, magical effects and Soul Strain; none of these newly suggested numeric progressions is approved yet. New test proposal for specifically Full Heavy Adamantite: QI–QV AR **90/105/120/140/165**, quality requirement factors **1/.95/.90/.85/.80**, possible ordinary melee F factors **1/1.01/1.02/1.03/1.04** (F squared in Attack Power). Avoid asserting identical AR quality deltas across Boughsteel, Trufula, weapons and shields or that quality removes real Adamantite weight/SPD burden. Owner `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, approval notes `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.


## November 8, 2026 — Quality's dual physical benefits APPROVED; Adamantite AR ladder approved for testing

**LATEST EXPLICIT APPROVAL:** Physical Quality I/II/III/IV/V lowers item-appropriate STR/DEX/support requirements by **0/5/10/15/20%** and improves performance, without free magical attribute bonuses or loss of real mass/armor mobility. Conventional melee's **single** applicable attack-rating **F** is multiplied by **1/1.01/1.02/1.03/1.04**, resulting in up to ×1.0816 Power because Power is quadratic. Armor craftsmanship may increase actual covered **AR**, shield craftsmanship the independent **Shield Rating**; do not add these to the wearer's END. **Approved working Full Heavy Adamantite AR I–V ladder: 90/105/120/140/165**, retaining Quality V target **155–180** as approximate balance goal, not final universally applied material statistics. Adamantite full suit STR113 and −30% inherent SPD still *material test candidates*; other material/class AR ladders, shield quality numeric factors, per-shape F and ranged engineering bonuses unresolved. Applying QV quality savings to a hypothetical STR113 material requirement gives **ceil(113×0.80)=91** before any separately qualifying synergy. Mundane quality does not automatically confer separate equipment stat enhancements, free rune benefits, Soul Strain exemptions, added attacks or eliminate inherent SPD burden. Re-test against quality-matched weapons and Hero-level stats before finalizing. Owning reference `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, approval `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.


## November 8, 2026 — Rounded QV Adamantite AR170 and Boughsteel I–V approved working targets

**LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL SUPERSEDES EARLIER AR165/166 ENDPOINT:** The current **approved working** Full Heavy **Adamantite Quality I–V AR ladder is 90/105/120/140/170**. AR170 (not 165 or suggested 166) is the preferred rounded Quality V target. **Approved working Full Heavy Boughsteel Quality I–V AR ladder is 75/85/95/105/115**. These two ladders are endorsed **calibration references**, not finished general material-specific armor canon or authority to assume the same additions for other armor classes. Approved mundane craftsmanship Quality I–V physical handling requirement multipliers **1/.95/.90/.85/.80** and melee single-F multipliers **1/1.01/1.02/1.03/1.04** continue unchanged; inherent SPD burden is separate and special material STR/AR/handling values need full design. **Trufula draft trial only, NOT APPROVED:** Full Heavy Quality I–V AR **60/70/80/90/100** against tentative full-ensemble physical support STR23 and inherent SPD −12%; quality-adjusted QV STR19 and worn SPD30.8 for unpenalized SPD35 wearer. Comparison QV Boughsteel AR115, QV Adamantite AR170, theoretical SPD28/24.5 if qualified; level100 QV STR-led Boughsteel hand axe strike ~34,970.87 Power versus END139 gives Slashing raw impact ratios **1.193 / 1.074 / 0.725** in that order. Trufula ×4 Fire weakness, persistent combustion and future extinguishers unchanged; Lightning ×3 remains tentative. Mixed material geometry, lower-level qualification, final material AR/SPD/STR, price and form-specific injury adjustment still require author review. See `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md` and `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.


## November 8, 2026 — All three Full Heavy quality AR ladders accepted as working tests; composites under review

**LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL:** Full Heavy **Trufula Quality I–V main-surface AR 60/70/80/90/100 is now APPROVED WORKING CALIBRATION**, joining approved working Full Heavy Boughsteel **75/85/95/105/115** and Adamantite **90/105/120/140/170**. These material/quality AR targets remain playtest values, not immutable global formulas. The previously discussed hypothetical physical support STR and intrinsic SPD modifiers of the three materials are **still unapproved** (Full Heavy conventional −20% SPD already an endorsed reference). Trufula Fire ×4 weakness / difficult-to-extinguish combustion remain; Lightning ×3 is merely likely/tentative. **USER DIRECTS: proceed to mixed-material armor review.** Current noncanon sample designs are engineered Quality V Full Heavy Trufula/Boughsteel **AR105, adjusted STR28, inherent −16% SPD** and Trufula/Adamantite **AR135, adjusted STR55, inherent −21% SPD**, subject to author review, actual hit-location materials/functional protection, and full outfit carrying burden. Do not derive or stack best-of-material bonuses. See `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md` and `10_Canon_Changelog.md`.


## November 8, 2026 — Trufula composite conductive-shock hazard and magical quenching APPROVED

**LATEST AUTHOR COMPOSITE RULE:** Use functional-component local armor protection and a single real whole-ensemble support-STR/SPD result, not the best rating of every component and not additive independent AR layers. Previous two QV Full Heavy mixed benchmark profiles are now **approved working comparisons**, NOT universal recipes: significant Trufula frame/Boughsteel panel **AR105, adjusted STR28, inherent −16% SPD**, and Trufula support/Adamantite components **AR135, STR55, −21% SPD**. Pure Trufula's **×4 Fire weakness** remains; meaningful Trufula-containing **composite armor has ×2 Fire weakness**, subject to real exposure and protected-vs-exposed assembly. If the composite has a genuine conductive route delivering Lightning through a metal/conductive component to Trufula, use **×3 Lightning weakness** with potential Trufula ignition. This specific **conductive-composite ×3 is approved**, while pure and adequately insulating configurations' precise Lightning susceptibility remain unfinalized. Ordinary natural-water dousing alone cannot extinguish a Trufula blaze; **appropriate specialized potions, powerfully directed Wakecraft manipulating existing water, or high-end ice magic can extinguish it**, with spell-resource/MP/strength/ignition thresholds not numerically set. No guaranteed ignition or automatic elemental wound is created by conduction. Relevant owning files `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`, `07_Centaurs_and_Smithcraft.md`, `13_Wakecraft_The_Six_Arts_of_the_Living_Tide.md`, `10_Canon_Changelog.md`. Pure Full Heavy quality AR ladders remain Trufula 60/70/80/90/100, Boughsteel 75/85/95/105/115, Adamantite 90/105/120/140/170; item-stat and SPD material general formulas unresolved. **NEXT:** extend composite protection calibration beyond Full Heavy without inventing universal material averaging or assuming shield rules automatically mirror body armor.

## Ancient true Serpentari aquatic physiology and planetary waterways — November 9, 2026

**AUTHOR-APPROVED CANON:** Ancient true Serpentari are naturally aquatic colossal serpents, capable of humanoid transformation. Their Imperium constructed immense **Serpentari-exclusive water passages spanning Mystaria** and penetrating its crust. Proposals that some reach the core remain speculative. The **Scaled City** (an allegedly slave-free secret Serpentari refuge, possibly inhabited by surviving Serpentari) is a **rumor, not confirmed geography**. The network's proper name awaits DM choice; proposed Worldcoil, Deepveins, Sovereign Channels, Undercoil, Abyssal Arteries. Controlling dossier: [`34_Ancient_Serpentari_Aquatic_Waterways_Canon.md`](34_Ancient_Serpentari_Aquatic_Waterways_Canon.md). The new aquatic traits apply to ancient true Serpentari, not automatically to the distinct modern Untouched Serpentari body plan.

**Campaign-specific:** The Tidekin Legend **Tala Sixwake** and her Meridian's confiscation into a Serpentari-era kobold vault are now approved planning canon. For future loot/treasure generation, **always consult [`campaigns/slate/Meridian_Loot_Clue_Register.md`](campaigns/slate/Meridian_Loot_Clue_Register.md)**, especially the intentionally preserved Mirrorwake-readable water vial and the damaged kobold research notebook needing translation/restoration. Neither is yet confirmed as found in live play.


## Finalized names and Elder Star vigil — November 9, 2026

**CANON UPDATE:** The planetary Serpentari underwater network is named **the Worldcoil**, with the deeper, extraordinarily dangerous **Undercoil** reserved for epic/high-level threats. The modern Naga Imperium still uses controlled sections of the Worldcoil and aquatic Starspawn can be unusually aware of it. A company of **Elder Starspawn** continues its original God War mission at the eventual final Undercoil approaches, with repeated Celestial Recalls and returns to battle; the **Scaled City remains an unverified legend** and no exact combat content is settled. See [`34_Ancient_Serpentari_Aquatic_Waterways_Canon.md`](34_Ancient_Serpentari_Aquatic_Waterways_Canon.md).

**SLATE RP PREP:** [`campaigns/slate/Next_Three_Scenes_DM_Packet.md`](campaigns/slate/Next_Three_Scenes_DM_Packet.md) has proposed vault rewards (two additional kobold/Serpentari curiosities), Dusk hideout continuity and the first Mara recruit mission. Proposed objects are **not yet approved or collected**. The Mirrorwake water vial and damaged kobold research notebook are retained as future clue seeds.
